### [201] [Repair or Resample? Rethinking Failure Debugging in LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25920)
*Zhongwen Luan,Xiaoyu Zhang,Ming Hu,Yue Yang,Jiongchi Yu,Xiaohong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SymTrace评估框架和SymFail数据集，揭示了现有LLM多智能体系统（MAS）的无引导重运行修复方法主要依赖随机性而非因果修复（修复率仅6.90%），并证明症状驱动干预方法能将修复率提升至20.15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM-based MAS在长程复杂任务中的应用增加，其可靠性成为部署瓶颈。现有调试方法依赖重运行和重采样，但尚不清楚这些方法是因果性地修复了故障，还是仅仅利用了LLM采样的随机性进行随机修复。

Method: 1. 提出SymTrace：一个受控评估框架，通过记录执行轨迹和建立干预锚点，在重放时重构锚点前的执行并仅再生下游轨迹，以可靠复现故障。
2. 构建SymFail数据集：包含536个人工标注的故障轨迹，含图链接位置、类别和追踪证据。
3. 在三个主流MAS框架上进行大规模实证研究，对比无引导重运行方法与症状驱动干预方法的效果。

Result: 1. 现有无引导重运行方法不可靠，故障复现率仅为67.97%，修复率仅为6.90%。
2. 症状驱动干预方法成功修复了20.15%的失败案例，相比最先进修复方法提升了191.89%。

Conclusion: 现有MAS修复方法主要依赖随机性而非因果修复，可靠性低；症状驱动的干预方法能显著提高修复效果，为MAS调试研究提供了可操作的见解，有助于推动多智能体系统的稳健部署。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MASs) are increasingly applied to long-horizon complex tasks, their reliability has emerged as the core bottleneck hindering their real-world deployment. Existing MAS debugging and repair methods typically rely on rerunning and resampling the entire execution trajectory. However, a fundamental question remains to be answered: do these methods causally repair MAS failures or merely stochastically repair by leveraging the randomness of LLM sampling? To evaluate the effectiveness of MAS repair methods, we introduce SymTrace, a controlled evaluation framework that records the MAS execution trajectory and establishes intervention anchors. During replay, it effectively reconstructs the execution before the anchor using recorded logs and only regenerates the downstream trajectory, thereby enabling the reliable reproduction of MAS failures. We further construct the dataset SymFail, comprising 536 human-annotated failure trajectories with graph-linked locations, categories, and trace evidence. Based on these foundations, we conduct a large-scale empirical study across three mainstream MAS frameworks. Our findings reveal that existing unguided rerun methods are highly unreliable, exhibiting low failure reproduction and repair rates (only 67.97% and 6.90%, respectively). Building upon these findings, we further explore the effectiveness of a symptom-driven intervention method, which successfully repairs 20.15% of the failed cases (a 191.89% improvement to state-of-the-art repair methods). This study aims to provide actionable insights for MAS debugging and repair research, paving the way for the robust deployment of multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [202] [Formal, Executable and Explainable Runtime Monitoring of Spoken Air Traffic Control Operational Procedures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25926)
*Roberto Luvini,Giacomo Longo,Alessandro Armando,Enrico Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种运行时验证框架，通过监控管制员与飞行员之间的无线电通信、监视数据和机载观察，来评估空中交通管制指令是否被执行，从而提升航空安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中交通管制依赖管制员与飞行员之间的语音交流，执行失败可能导致严重安全隐患甚至致命事故，因此需要一种方法来评估指令是否被正确执行。

Method: 构建运行时验证框架，将无线电通信解析为与实体关联的事件，并与监视数据和机载观察合并为带时间戳的轨迹；将ICAO规定的义务形式化为带明确时间界限的时序公式，并在执行轨迹上进行评估。

Result: 在真实交通数据中，完整流程对人工标注违规的F1分数达到0.85；在1,495个合成情境中，监控逻辑均返回预期结果；在两起历史事故重建中，监控器识别出与调查报告一致的程序偏差。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效监控空中交通管制程序，自动识别指令执行中的违规行为，有助于提升航空运输安全。

Abstract: Air traffic control procedures are executed through spoken exchanges between controllers and pilots. These interactions are essential to the safety of air transportation: failures in their execution can create severe operational hazards, as evidenced by past fatal accidents. Assessing whether an instruction has been followed requires relating what was said to the aircraft concerned, its state, and the obligations that pilots must meet. We present a runtime verification framework that monitors such procedures by checking controller-pilot exchanges, surveillance data, and onboard observations. The framework parses radio communications into events linked to the entities they concern and merges them with surveillance and onboard observations into a time-stamped trace. The ICAO-derived obligations as formalized as temporal formulas with explicit time bounds and evaluated over execution traces. Every violation is reported along with the breached obligations and the observations that support the verdict. With real traffic, the complete pipeline reaches an F1 of 0.85 against blind human-annotated violations; in 1,495 synthetic situations derived from two public corpora, the monitor logic returns the expected verdict in every case. In two historical accidents reconstructed from official investigation reports, the monitor identifies the same procedural deviations documented by the investigators.

</details>


### [203] [How Robust Are Automated Fact-Checking Systems? A Cross-Benchmark Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25934)
*Aida Usmanova,Zangir Iklassov,Markus Leippold,Ricardo Usbeck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对自动化事实核查（AFC）系统进行了跨数据集的全面基准测试，发现现有系统泛化能力差，检索是主要瓶颈，且模型排名高度依赖领域和评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AFC研究缺乏简单基线对比，系统通常针对单一基准开发，缺乏跨领域泛化能力的评估，且没有对完整的“检索-验证”两阶段流程进行跨数据集的交叉评估。

Method: 在四个涵盖科学、开放网络和气候领域的数据集上，对九种模型进行基准测试，包括随机/稀疏基线、微调Transformer、零样本LLM以及AVeriTeC 2025共享任务中排名最高的两个系统。

Result: 1) 在ClimateCheck上，仅使用声明的微调模型优于零样本LLM和顶级AVeriTeC系统，表明噪声证据会降低预测准确性；2) 系统排名强烈依赖于领域和指标，最佳模型在不同数据集上表现差异巨大；3) 用黄金标注替换检索证据可将准确性提高14-22个百分点，证实检索是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 当前AFC系统泛化能力有限，检索阶段是性能瓶颈，未来研究需关注跨领域鲁棒性和检索质量提升，作者已开源代码和数据以支持可重复研究。

Abstract: Automated fact-checking (AFC) systems retrieve evidence and predict claim veracity, yet evaluations omit simple baselines, systems are developed for a single benchmark and cannot be trusted to generalise across domains. No prior work cross-evaluates the full two-stage retrieve-then-verify pipeline across diverse datasets, complementing retrieval-only studies (Thakur et al., 2021) and single-stage benchmarking studies (Calamai et al., 2025). We benchmark nine models, ranging from random and sparse baselines to fine-tuned transformers, zero-shot LLMs, and the two highest-ranked systems from the AVeriTeC 2025 shared task, across four datasets spanning scientific, open-web, and climate domains. Three findings stand out: (1) on ClimateCheck claim-only and fine-tuned models outperform zero-shot LLM and top-performing AVeriTeC 2025 systems, highlighting that noisy evidence can degrade veracity prediction; (2) system rankings are strongly domain- and metric-dependent: the best model on SciFact (macro-F1 0.70) drops to 0.31 on ClimateCheck, while the AVeriTeC 2025 winner and runner-up swap rankings based on evaluation metrics and datasets; (3) replacing retrieved evidence with gold annotations improves veracity accuracy by 14-22 points across models, confirming retrieval remains primary bottleneck. We release code, pre-processed datasets, and all results to support reproducible AFC research.

</details>


### [204] [Candidate supply and answer selection shape the value of LLM judging in multi-agent systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25937)
*Jia-Hao Ji,Sijie Li,Jiabei Cheng,Zixi She,Jin-Tai Yu,Zhiyuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多智能体系统（MAS）常因缺乏质量控制导致“模因漂移”，即正确答案虽已生成却被错误答案覆盖。本文引入LLM法官作为选择压力，发现结合答案频率与法官评估可显著提升准确率（从63.82%升至约70.9%），主要挽救了被流行错误淹没的正确答案，为设计保护正确生成的MAS架构提供了诊断基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多智能体系统中“正确答案已生成但最终报告错误答案”的悖论。现有系统难以解释此现象，因为生成、沟通和最终选择规则通常同时变化，导致共识在缺乏质量控制时出现“模因漂移”（memetic drift），即错误答案因流行度而非正确性被选中。

Method: 将多智能体推理概念化为包含候选生成、同行沟通和终端选择的进化流水线。研究两个核心问题：(1) LLM法官何时能通过提供正确性信号施加有效的选择压力；(2) 使用该信号何时能改善最终报告的答案。实验涵盖对MMLU-Pro等五个基准共15,336个问题的法官可靠性分析，以及在16,278个问题衍生的81,390个固定候选池上的规则回放测试，通过隔离生成、识别和选择环节进行验证。

Result: (1) 正确答案常存在于候选池中，但系统仍会收敛于错误答案；(2) 法官可靠性非模型固有特质，而是随任务、生成器及正确答案稀有度动态变化；(3) 将答案频率与法官评估结合仅改变最终选择规则，即将准确率从63.82%提升至70.82%-70.95%，主要机制是挽救了数量上被流行错误压倒的正确答案。

Conclusion: 在多智能体系统中，生成更多候选值的价值取决于这些额外样本是否使正确答案变得“存在”、“频繁”或“可识别”。通过隔离生成、识别和选择过程，本研究确立了设计多智能体架构的诊断基础，旨在保护已生成的正确答案不被丢失。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) sometimes already have the potential to answer correctly, but still report a wrong answer. Explaining this outcome is difficult because generation, communication and final answer-selection rules usually change simultaneously. We conceptualize multi-agent reasoning as an evolutionary pipeline of candidate generation, peer communication and terminal selection, wherein consensus without quality control can exhibit patterns of memetic drift. We study two questions: (1) when an LLM judge provides effective selection pressure by supplying a signal of answer correctness for candidates generated in a multi-agent system, and (2) when using that signal improves the reported answer. To map judge reliability, we analysed 15,336 questions from MMLU-Pro, GPQA, MedXpertQA and MuSR, with Humanity's Last Exam analysed separately. To test these rules, we replayed 81,390 fixed candidate pools drawn from 16,278 questions across five benchmarks. We report three findings. (1) A correct answer is often already present among the generated candidates, but the system can still converge on and report a wrong answer. (2) Judge reliability is not a fixed trait of the model, but varies with the task, the generator and how rare the correct answer is. (3) Combining answer frequency with the judge's evaluation changed only the final answer-selection rule and raised accuracy from 63.82% to 70.82-70.95%, primarily by rescuing correct answers that were outnumbered by popular errors. In the systems studied here, the value of generating more candidates depends on whether those extra samples make correct answers present, frequent or recognisable. By isolating generation, recognition and selection, these findings establish a diagnostic basis for designing multi-agent architectures that protect generated correct answers from being lost.

</details>


### [205] [LivingRAG: Augmenting Graph RAG with Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25960)
*Yuzhuo Cui,Zongye Zhang,Qingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LivingRAG 是一个图 RAG 框架，通过引入可写入和可复用的推理经验存储，改进多跳问答任务，提升检索效率和答案生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于图的 RAG 系统通常孤立处理每个查询，并在推理后丢弃 LLM 的有用推理信息，导致后续相关查询需重新检索和推理，效率低下。

Method: 在图检索主干上添加可写入的经验存储，将已验证的经验以图信号和摘要形式存储，在推理时复用：图信号辅助检索相关实体和段落，摘要提供参考推理模式。

Result: 在多跳 QA 基准测试中，LivingRAG 相比强 RAG 基线提升了准确率，并在复用相关先验经验时减少了完成 token 的使用量。

Conclusion: 通过引入可复用的推理经验机制，LivingRAG 有效提升了图 RAG 系统在连续查询场景下的性能与效率，为动态知识利用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Graph-based RAG improves multi-hop question answering by organizing evidence as a knowledge graph. However, most existing RAG systems process each query in isolation and discard useful reasoning from the LLM's response after inference. As a result, later related queries need to retrieve evidence and reason from scratch. We propose LivingRAG, a Graph RAG framework with writable and reusable reasoning experience. LivingRAG adds a writable experience store to a graph-based retrieval backbone, enabling verified experiences to be reused during inference in two ways. Stored graph signals help retrieval find entities and passages that were useful in earlier related queries. Stored summaries provide a reference reasoning pattern for answer generation. We analyze online QA streams and find reusable signals from shared entities, graph neighborhoods, and question templates. Experiments on multi-hop QA benchmarks show that LivingRAG improves accuracy over strong RAG baselines and reduces completion-token use when relevant prior experience is reused.

</details>


### [206] [Quantitative Analysis of $ω$-Regular Robust MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25968)
*Ali Asadi,Krishnendu Chatterjee,Ehsan Kafshdar Goharshady,Mehrdad Karrabi,Alipasha Montaseri,Ali Shafiee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性定义不确定性集的(s,a)-矩形鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程(RMDPs)中的奇偶目标问题，提出了精确的定量分析方法和多项式时间算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典MDP允许转移概率存在不确定性，RMDPs通过优化最坏情况实现来扩展这一模型。先前的工作主要集中在定性分析（几乎肯定或正概率问题），而本文旨在解决精确的定量问题，即在所有环境策略下保证满足概率的上确界。

Method: 1. 证明智能体和环境都存在纯无记忆最优策略；2. 为线性定义鲁棒马尔可夫链上的定量奇偶问题提供多项式时间算法，并将其作为RMDPs策略迭代算法的子程序；3. 结合定量单步改进和定性几乎肯定改进。

Result: 提出了针对线性定义不确定性集RMDPs中奇偶目标的精确定量求解方法，并通过实验验证了该方法与显式归约为随机博弈方法相比的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功解决了线性定义不确定性集RMDPs中的精确定量奇偶目标问题，提供了理论保证和高效算法，并通过实验验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Robust Markov Decision Processes (RMDPs) generalize classical MDPs by allowing uncertainty in transition probabilities and optimizing against their worst-case realization. We consider $(s,a)$-rectangular RMDPs with \emph{linearly defined} uncertainty sets and study parity objectives, which are a canonical representation of $ω$-regular objectives. An uncertainty set is linearly defined if it is described by linear inequalities over the transition distribution together with auxiliary variables, which capture the standard $L_1$ and $L_\infty$ balls as well as general polytopic uncertainty sets.
  The quantitative value is the supremum, over all agent policies, of the satisfaction probability guaranteed against the adversarial environment. Previous work studied the qualitative analysis, namely the almost-sure (resp. positive) problem that asks whether a single agent policy guarantees satisfaction with probability one (resp. positive probability) against every environment policy. In this work, we solve the exact quantitative problem.
  Our contributions are threefold. First, we show that both the agent and the environment admit pure memoryless optimal policies. Second, we give a polynomial-time algorithm for quantitative parity on linearly defined robust Markov chains and use it as a subroutine in a policy-iteration algorithm for RMDPs. The algorithm combines quantitative one-step improvements with qualitative almost-sure improvements. Finally, we report experiments comparing our approach with the explicit reduction to stochastic games.

</details>


### [207] [SciMIF: Understanding Multimodal Instruction Following in Scientific Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25973)
*Ye Shen,Yuting Zheng,Dun Pei,Zijian Chen,Wenlong Zhang,Qi Jia,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 SciMIF 基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在科学领域遵循复杂指令的能力，发现化学领域挑战最大且模型规模增加并未显著提升约束遵循能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学领域中理解指令遵循能力对于有效利用多模态大语言模型推动科学发展至关重要，但目前缺乏针对科学领域多模态指令遵循的评估基准。

Method: 基于对 5 个代表性科学学科中 22 个不同任务的广泛分析，提出包含 10 个约束组的综合分类法，并开发高保真指令注入管道来系统增强现有科学数据集，在多个最先进的闭源和开源多模态大语言模型上进行实验。

Result: 发现不同科学学科之间存在显著性能差异，化学领域对当前模型挑战最大；增加模型规模并未带来约束遵循能力的相应提升；当前模型在细粒度约束和需要深度应用学科知识的指令上表现严重不足。

Conclusion: SciMIF 填补了科学领域多模态指令遵循评估的空白，为未来在严谨科学应用中增强多模态大语言模型奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: Understanding instruction-following capabilities in scientific domains is essential for effectively leveraging Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to advance the development of scientific fields. In this work, we introduce SciMIF, a novel benchmark designed to evaluate the capability of MLLMs in following complex scientific instructions. Specifically, based on an extensive analysis of 22 distinct tasks across 5 representative scientific disciplines, we propose a comprehensive taxonomy comprising 10 constraint groups that captures both general functional requirements and discipline-specific characteristics. Guided by this taxonomy, we develop a high-fidelity instruction injection pipeline to systematically augment existing scientific datasets. We conduct comprehensive experiments on multiple state-of-the-art closed-source and open-source MLLMs. Our findings reveal significant performance disparities across different scientific disciplines, with chemistry posing greater challenges for current MLLMs. Furthermore, we observe that increasing the model scale does not yield corresponding improvements in constraint adherence, and current models still struggle severely with fine-grained constraints and instructions requiring the deep application of disciplinary knowledge. SciMIF fills the current void in evaluating multimodal instruction adherence within scientific domains, laying a crucial foundation for future enhancements of MLLMs in rigorous scientific applications. Data and code will be released at https://github.com/shenye7436/SciMIF .

</details>


### [208] [Multi-Granularity Context-Enhanced RAG over Multimodal Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25986)
*Zongyu Wu,Yilong Wang,Xiaochen Wang,Minhua Lin,Zhichao Xu,Fenglong Ma,Xiang Zhang,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种上下文增强的多模态知识图谱（CEMMKG）框架，通过在局部和全局范围内为图像补充文本上下文，解决了现有方法中图文语义鸿沟问题，显著提升了多模态GraphRAG的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于多模态知识图谱（MMKG）的检索增强生成（RAG）方法通常独立处理不同模态后再进行融合，导致视觉信息提取过程中对文本上下文的利用不足，造成图像与文本之间的语义鸿沟，限制了多模态GraphRAG的性能。

Method: 提出构建上下文增强多模态知识图谱（CEMMKG）框架：1. 在局部范围引入多粒度设计，不仅利用周围文本，还纳入与图像语义相关的句子；2. 在全局范围提供整段文本的摘要；3. 通过上述机制为每个图像 enrich 互补的文本上下文，以缩小模态间的语义差距。

Result: 在以视觉为中心的数据集上进行的广泛实验表明，CEMMKG能有效利用上下文信息提升MMKG-based RAG的性能，且在不同MMKG-based RAG方法中均表现出良好的通用性和适用性。

Conclusion: CEMMKG通过局部多粒度和全局文本上下文的增强，有效弥合了多模态数据中的语义鸿沟，为构建更高效的多模态GraphRAG系统提供了新的解决方案，具有广泛的适用价值。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is widely used to mitigate hallucination issues in large language models (LLMs) and multimodal large language models (MLLMs). In particular, knowledge graph (KG)-based RAG leverages structured knowledge to provide (M)LLMs with high-quality external information. Building on these works, recent studies have explored multimodal knowledge graphs (MMKGs) as knowledge bases for GraphRAG. This enables Graph RAG to integrate knowledge across multiple modalities, thereby further enhancing its performance. However, existing MMKG-based RAG methods generally follow a common pipeline in which different modalities are largely processed independently before being fusion. As a result, textual context is only used to a limited extent during visual information extraction and subsequent multimodal knowledge fusion. This brings a semantic gap between images and text which limits the multimodal GraphRAG performance. To address this issue, we propose a novel framework for constructing a Context-Enhanced MMKG (CEMMKG) to better support multimodal GraphRAG. The proposed CEMMKG enriches each image with complementary textual context at both local and global scopes. Local context goes beyond the surrounding text by incorporating sentences that are semantically related to the image, while global context provides a summary of the entire passage. We further introduce a multi-granularity design for the local context, allowing it to capture semantically relevant information at different levels of detail. Extensive experiments on the selected vision-centric dataset validate that CEMMKG is effective in leveraging contextual information to improve MMKG-based RAG performance. Moreover, its effectiveness across different MMKG-based RAG methods demonstrates its broad applicability.

</details>


### [209] [ProgRouter: Online Progress-Guided Orchestration for Multi-Agent LLM Workflows under Quality-Cost Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25992)
*Somgyuan Li,Ahmed M. Abdelmoniem,Shiqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ProgRouter，一种在线进度引导的路由框架，用于多代理 LLM 工作流，通过自适应选择 LLM 代理来降低运营成本，同时保持任务解决质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多代理 LLM 工作流因重复调用 LLM 和长上下文积累导致运营成本高昂；现有级联路由方法无法适应多步工作流的动态、状态依赖特性。

Method: 引入多视图任务进度评分器，结合粗粒度工作流结果状态与细粒度子任务完成信号、进度趋势及工作流状态质量；采用双路径任务进度预测器和自适应元门控机制估计候选 LLM 的进度增益，实现在线逐步路由决策。

Result: 在 HumanEval Plus、MBPP、MATH-500 和 ASQA 上的实验表明，ProgRouter 相比关键基线显著降低运营成本，同时保持较强的任务解决性能。

Conclusion: ProgRouter 通过平衡进度增益、任务时间预算和长期运营效率，有效解决了多代理 LLM 工作流中的成本与性能权衡问题。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) workflows have emerged as a powerful paradigm for solving complex, open-ended tasks through collaborative reasoning among specialized LLM agents, but they incur substantial operating costs due to repeated LLM invocations and long-horizon context accumulation. Existing cascade routing methods make one-shot, query-level decisions and cannot adapt to the dynamic, state-dependent nature of multi-step workflows, in which the right LLM at each step depends on evolving task progress, remaining task difficulty, and cost-efficiency requirements. We present ProgRouter, an online progress-guided routing framework that adaptively selects LLM agents across workflow steps to preserve task-solving quality while adhering to time and cost budgets. ProgRouter introduces a multi-view task progress scorer that combines coarse workflow outcome regimes with fine-grained signals on subtask completion, progress trends, and workflow state quality. Then, a dual-path task progress predictor and an adaptive meta-gating mechanism estimate the progress gain for each candidate routed LLM. ProgRouter makes online step-wise routing decisions that balance progress gain, task time budgets, and long-term operating cost efficiency. Experiments on HumanEval Plus, MBPP, MATH-500, and ASQA, spanning agentic code generation, mathematical reasoning, and retrieval-augmented long-form question answering, demonstrate that ProgRouter reduces the operating cost relative to key baselines while maintaining strong task-solving performance.

</details>


### [210] [AsymSpec: Context-Asymmetric Speculative Decoding for Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26004)
*Sheng Liang,Yongyue Zhang,Nathanael Brian,Hang Lv,Hao Wang,Chen Zhang,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AsymSpec 提出了一种非对称推测解码框架，通过轻量级 Draft 模型读取完整输入、大型验证模型使用压缩输入，在保持约 90% 全上下文准确率的同时，实现 1.3-1.7 倍吞吐量提升和 0.2-0.3 倍计算成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic LLM 管道因检索、工具使用和多轮交互导致上下文累积，推理成本不断上升。现有部署通过压缩输入控制延迟但会降低任务准确率；推测解码（SD）虽能无损加速生成，但要求 Draft 和验证模型共享相同上下文，无法解决准确率与开销的权衡问题。

Method: 提出 AsymSpec 非对称推测解码框架：轻量级 Draft 模型读取完整输入，大型验证模型在压缩视图上运行；通过对比δ-融合 logits 引导验证模型，并由发散感知接受门控调节，以保持验证稳定性和高 Draft 接受率。

Result: 在四个代理能力和两个端到端代理基准测试中，AsymSpec 平均达到约 90% 的全上下文准确率，在孤立文本能力上实现 1.3-1.7 倍吞吐量加速，计算成本仅为 0.2-0.3 倍。

Conclusion: 非对称上下文访问在压缩丢弃关键推理信号时能产生显著收益，有效解决了准确率与计算开销之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Agentic LLM pipelines face escalating inference costs as context accumulates across retrieval, tool use, and multi-turn interactions. To control latency, deployments routinely compress inputs, but this degrades task accuracy. Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates generation losslessly, yet it assumes the drafter and verifier share an identical context, preventing SD from resolving the accuracy-overhead trade-off. We propose AsymSpec, an asymmetric speculative decoding framework that breaks this symmetry: a lightweight drafter reads the full input while the large verifier operates on the compressed view. The drafter steers the verifier via a contrastive $δ$-fusion of logits, modulated by a divergence-aware acceptance gate that preserves verification stability and high draft acceptance rates. Evaluated across four agentic capabilities and two end-to-end agent benchmarks, AsymSpec reaches $\approx 90\%$ of full-context accuracy on average, delivering $1.3$--$1.7\times$ throughput speedups at $0.2$--$0.3\times$ the compute cost on isolated text capabilities. These results show that asymmetric context access yields substantial gains precisely when compression discards critical reasoning signals.

</details>


### [211] [Imitation Learning for Connection-Tableau Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26009)
*Fredrik Rømming,Mantas Bakšys,Martin S. Fixman,Sean B. Holden*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络的自动定理证明策略，通过模仿学习从已有证明中训练策略，在多个基准测试上比传统方法leanCoP多解决46%的问题，且证明步骤减少一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动定理证明需要在每一步选择添加或移除什么内容，传统方法依赖符号回溯搜索，效率有限。作者希望将证明构建视为策略在形式演算诱导的转移系统中的行为，利用策略学习方法提升证明效率。

Method: 将clausal connection tableaux的搜索和规划统一为状态化策略，使用图神经网络对证明编辑进行评分，通过模仿学习从已找到的证明中训练策略，并逐步减少搜索辅助（从完整符号回溯到纯策略驱动）来测试性能。

Result: 在M2k、MPTP2078-bushy和TPTP v9.2.1固定步数预算下，学习到的策略比leanCoP多解决高达46%的问题，且达到证明所需的步骤减少了一个数量级。

Conclusion: 基于图神经网络的策略学习方法能有效提升自动定理证明的效率和成功率，证明了将证明构建视为策略学习问题的可行性，且策略可在减少搜索辅助的情况下仍保持良好性能。

Abstract: An automated theorem prover builds a proof step by step, choosing at each point what to add and what to remove. We cast this construction as a policy acting in a transition system induced by a formal calculus, which fixes which steps are sound: for clausal connection tableaux, leanCoP-style search and plCoP/rlCoP-style planning then become stateful policies over one interface, and policy-learning methods apply directly. We equip such policies with a graph neural network that scores proof edits from structure that transfers across problems, train it by imitation learning from found proofs, and measure how performance holds as we remove search scaffolding, from full symbolic backtracking to a policy the network drives alone. Within a fixed step budget on M2k, MPTP2078-bushy, and TPTP v9.2.1, learned policies solve up to 46% more problems than leanCoP, and reach proofs in an order of magnitude fewer steps.

</details>


### [212] [Trace Integrity for LLM Data Agents: A Vision for Auditable Structured Reasoning in Real-World Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26036)
*Srimonti Dutta,Akshata Kishore Moharir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出仅凭答案准确率不足以评估LLM数据代理的可靠性，提出了“追踪完整性”（Trace Integrity）作为新的部署可靠性标准，并定义了“结构差距”（Structure Gap）问题。通过引入执行契约和CAIT指标，实证表明高准确率可能掩盖无效的推理过程，主张应同时评估答案及其背后的可审计计算过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决结构化数据任务中“正确答案/无效追踪”（CAIT）的问题，即模型可能通过无效的推理路径得出基准正确的答案。现有的自然语言推理和自由形式的理由无法可靠地指定真实系统所需的算子级程序，导致部署失败（结构差距），因此需要一种能评估计算过程显式性、可执行性和可审计性的新标准。

Method: 提出“追踪完整性”标准，要求计算记录具备显式、可执行、模式有效、算子忠实、可重放、答案一致及可审计等特征。通过“执行契约”将用户意图绑定到模式元素、算子计划、假设、可执行查询及验证状态。引入CAIT率指标来量化仅基于答案评估时将计算不支持的输出误判为成功的频率，并在BIRD Mini-Dev等数据集上进行实证对比。

Result: 在BIRD Mini-Dev上的实验显示，Direct SQL、Operation Summary + SQL和Contract-First SQL的答案准确率分别为20%、22%和24%，但其追踪完整性通过率显著更高（39%、43%、40%），且CAIT率居高不下（55%、59.1%、45.8%）。这证明答案准确率、追踪有效性和静默失败风险是三个截然不同的评估信号。

Conclusion: 现实世界的LLM数据代理不应仅通过输出是否匹配参考答案来评估，必须同时评估其输出是否由可审计的计算过程支持。追踪完整性是确保部署可靠性的关键，能有效识别仅靠答案准确率无法发现的计算缺陷。

Abstract: Answer accuracy is an insufficient reliability signal for LLM data agents. In structured-data tasks, a benchmark-correct answer can be produced by an invalid trace. This paper introduces Trace Integrity, a deployment reliability criterion for evaluating whether the computation recorded behind an answer is explicit, executable, schema-valid, operator-faithful, replayable, answer-consistent, and auditable. We identify the Structure Gap as the deployment failure mode that makes Trace Integrity necessary: natural-language reasoning and free-form rationales do not reliably specify the operator-level programs required by real-world systems. We operationalize Trace Integrity with execution contracts, structured artifacts that bind user intent to schema elements, operator plans, assumptions, executable queries, verification status, and final-answer linkage. We also introduce CAIT (Correct Answer / Invalid Trace) Rate, which measures how often answer-only evaluation counts computationally unsupported outputs as successes. In an empirical demonstration on BIRD Mini-Dev, Direct SQL, Operation Summary + SQL, and Contract-First SQL achieve answer accuracies of 20%, 22%, and 24%, while their Trace Integrity Pass Rates are 39%, 43%, and 40% and their CAIT Rates remain high at 55%, 59.1%, and 45.8%, showing that answer accuracy, trace validity, and silent-failure risk are distinct evaluation signals. Real-world LLM data agents should, therefore, be evaluated not only by whether their outputs match a reference answer, but by whether those outputs are backed by auditable computation.

</details>


### [213] [SwarmWorld: Stigmergic technological evolution in societies of language-model agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26081)
*Subhadeep Pal,Fiona Y. Wang,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SwarmWorld 框架，展示同质 LLM 智能体在无角色分配下通过环境交互自组织为技术社会，其协作产生的技术组合比独立搜索更广泛且具韧性，但最强单体发明仍由独立搜索产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索去中心化 LLM 智能体能否在无预定义角色或工作流下，通过共享环境协调构建功能性技术并超越独立搜索能力。

Method: 构建 SwarmWorld 模拟环境，同质 LLM 智能体在空间环境中探索、处理资源、测试材料、构建持久人工制品并编写可执行控制器，由确定性模拟器评估其在未知扰动下的功能表现。

Result: 共享社会 develops 更广泛、更具韧性的技术组合；智能体分化为探索、建造、维护和协调行为；技术通过协作建造、可执行继承和持久智能体 - 人工制品网络积累；物理观察是重用的主要起点。

Conclusion: 物理性痕迹（stigmergy）足以支持有能力的社会，而交互驱动持久技术生态而非普遍更优的个体发明；明确文化机制可放大协作，但功能效益取决于结果和时间尺度。

Abstract: Collective intelligence can emerge when individuals coordinate through a shared environment, allowing local actions to accumulate into durable social organization. Language-model agents offer a new substrate for this process, yet most multi-agent systems rely on direct conversation, predefined roles, or centralized workflows. It remains unclear whether decentralized agents can build functional technologies and outperform independent search. Here, initially homogeneous LLM agents in SwarmWorld self-organize without assigned roles or recipes into evolving technological societies. Agents explore a spatial environment, process resources, test materials, construct persistent artifacts, and write executable controllers evaluated by a deterministic simulator under unseen disturbances after the agents are removed. SwarmWorld splits cognition from consequence: agents propose architectures and controllers within fixed action and material schemas, while the simulated world determines function. Shared societies develop broader, more resilient technological portfolios than a strong best-of-N isolated-search baseline, although isolated search remains competitive for the strongest artifact. Agents differentiate into exploration, construction, maintenance, and coordination behaviors, transitioning as the world matures. Technologies accumulate through collaborative construction, executable inheritance, and persistent agent-artifact networks, with most reuse beginning through physical observation rather than communication. Explicit cultural mechanisms amplify collaboration and organization, but functional benefits depend on outcome and timescale. Physical stigmergy alone supports capable societies, while interaction drives persistent technological ecologies rather than universally superior individual inventions.

</details>


### [214] [Planetary Prediction Engine: Autonomous Geospatial Prediction via Intelligent Data Selection and Foundation Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26088)
*Evelyn Ma,Rama Kumar Pasumarthi,Kishwar Shafin,Mandar Sharma,Mimi Sun,Hamed Sadeghi,Dav M. Ebengo,Mbulayi Onesime,Rouslan Solomakhin,John Wamburu,William Ogallo,Aisha Walcott-Bryant,Sanxing Chen,Arbaaz Muslim,Yael Mayer,Ronald Ho,Roy Lee,Ruth Alcantara,Abdoulaye Diack,Monica Bharel,Lambert Rosique,Jeremy Amez-Droz,Christopher Haire,James Manyika,Yossi Matias,Niv Efron,Gautam Prasad,Shravya Shetty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了行星预测引擎（PPE），一个自主AI系统，可直接从自然语言查询执行端到端工作流，通过自动检索、融合多模态地理空间数据并优化模型架构，在多个任务中超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前构建预测性行星模型面临数据生态系统碎片化的瓶颈，需要手动数据检索、多模态数据策划与融合以及迭代模型选择，难以高效应对粮食安全、灾害风险、疾病爆发等全球性挑战。

Method: PPE系统从自然语言查询出发，自动检索时空相关协变量（来自Data Commons、Google Earth Engine等），融合地理空间基础模型嵌入（PDFM、AlphaEarth），并在任务定制模型架构族中搜索，同时配备自动过拟合防护机制。

Result: 在美国空间回归任务中，PPE在CDC健康指标、FEMA国家风险指数和社会脆弱性指数上分别将平均R²从60.0%、60.0%、58.6%提升至76.8%、64.9%、66.2%；在尼日利亚粮食安全指标空间降尺度任务中，R²从31.5%提升至66.1%；在2026年刚果民主共和国埃博拉疫情预测中，Recall@10达到83.3%，比现有最佳模型提升10.3个百分点。

Conclusion: PPE通过结合自主多模态行星数据发现与针对性模型优化，降低了行星尺度分析的技术门槛，实现了快速、定制化、专家级部署，为应对全球挑战提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Addressing critical global challenges, from food security and disaster risk to disease outbreaks and socio-economic vulnerability, demands high-fidelity geospatial modeling. However, building predictive planetary models remains bottlenecked by a fragmented data ecosystem, requiring manual data retrieval, multimodal data curation and fusion along with iterative model selection. We present the Planetary Prediction Engine (PPE), an autonomous AI system that executes this end-to-end workflow directly from natural-language queries. PPE synthesizes multimodal datasets on the fly, retrieving spatiotemporally relevant covariates across open-web and Earth observation platforms (Data Commons, Google Earth Engine) and fusing them with geospatial foundation model embeddings (PDFM, AlphaEarth). Simultaneously, it searches over task-tailored model architecture families with automated overfitting guards. Across diverse tasks, geographies, and scientific domains, PPE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art or manually tuned expert baselines. For US spatial regression, PPE improves mean $R^2$ across 21 CDC health indicators (76.8% vs. 60.0%), FEMA national risk indices (64.9% vs. 60.0%), and the Social Vulnerability Index (66.2% vs. 58.6%). For spatial downscaling in data-scarce settings, PPE integrates localized proxies to double baseline accuracy in Nigerian food security indicators ($R^2$ of 66.1% vs. 31.5%). For epidemiological nowcasting of the 2026 DRC Bundibugyo Ebola outbreak, PPE achieves a Recall@10 of 83.3% (identifying 15 of 18 newly invaded health zones across five weekly forecasts), a +10.3 percentage-point improvement over the public state-of-the-art modeling (~73%). By combining autonomous multimodal planetary data discovery with targeted model optimization, PPE lowers the technical barrier to planetary-scale analytics, enabling rapid, customized, expert-level deployment.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [215] [HCC+: Hyperbolic Guarding for Certified Attention Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24971)
*Liangchen Ge*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了双曲空间中注意力检索的 Lipschitz 稳定性，提出 HCC+ 框架，利用 Poincaré 球的三个特性实现确定性保障，并在存储效率上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在有限精度表示下缺乏对注意力权重保持的确定性保障。

Method: 引入 HCC+ 理论框架，利用 Poincaré 球的指数体积增长、双曲 1-中心的对数覆盖半径以及与嵌入维度无关的打包界这三个特性。

Result: 证明了两个确定性保障：精确检索时每层注意力偏差不超过理想值的 10%；软注意力时总变差距离以 O(1/√n) 衰减。框架实现了相对于 FP16 的 6.1 倍存储缩减。

Conclusion: 提供了非欧几何中首个确定性、查询无关的检索证书。

Abstract: We study the Lipschitz stability of attention retrieval in hyperbolic spaces. Existing methods lack deterministic guarantees on attention-weight preservation under finite-precision representations. We introduce HCC+, a theoretical framework exploiting three properties of the Poincaré ball: exponential volume growth enabling query-independent boundary truncation; logarithmic covering radius of hyperbolic 1-centers enabling dimension-independent critical-key identification; and a packing bound with constants independent of the embedding dimension. We prove two deterministic guarantees: for exact retrieval, the per-layer attention deviation is bounded by 10\% of its ideal value; for soft attention, the total variation distance decays as $O(1/\sqrt{n})$, the rate of finite-sample variance. As a consequence of the guarding mechanism, the framework achieves a storage reduction factor of $6.1\times$ relative to FP16. We provide the first deterministic, query-independent retrieval certificate in non-Euclidean geometry.

</details>


### [216] [Fuzzy Pattern Matching in Ordered Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25032)
*Armen Kostanyan,Arevik Harmandayan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于“轨迹”（trajectory）的统一框架，用于解决基本字母表序列及偏序集结构上的模糊模式匹配问题，该框架推广了KMP算法中的前缀函数概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模式匹配是计算机科学的基础问题，但现有的研究多集中于精确匹配或特定数据结构。为了处理更复杂的“模糊模式”（即基本字母表上的模糊属性序列），并将其应用范围从线性序列扩展到偏序集（partially ordered sets），需要一种通用的理论框架和算法机制。

Method: 引入“轨迹”（trajectory）这一核心概念，作为KMP算法中前缀函数的广义形式。通过将轨迹与相应的数据结构结合，将提出的算法建模为转换系统（transition systems）：对于序列，状态为轨迹；对于偏序结构，状态为与节点关联的轨迹。该方法分别针对序列寻找匹配段，针对偏序结构寻找匹配的饱和链（saturated chains）。

Result: 成功构建了适用于序列和偏序集两种不同数据结构的模糊模式匹配算法。该轨迹驱动的方法证明了其能够作为一个统一框架，有效处理不同结构下的模糊属性匹配问题。

Conclusion: 基于轨迹的方法为各种数据结构中的模糊模式匹配提供了一个统一且有效的理论框架，通过推广前缀函数概念，实现了从线性序列到复杂偏序结构的算法扩展。

Abstract: The problem of pattern matching, that is, finding all occurrences of a given pattern in a string, is one of the fundamental problems in computer science that has applications in many areas. In this paper, we consider fuzzy patterns, defined as sequences of fuzzy properties over the basic alphabet. We first consider fuzzy pattern matching for sequences of elements of the basic alphabet and then extend the problem to partially ordered sets of nodes labeled by elements of the basic alphabet. For sequences, we seek segments that match the pattern, whereas for partially ordered structures, we seek saturated chains of nodes that match the pattern. The key concept underlying the solutions to these problems is the notion of a trajectory, which generalizes the concept of the prefix function used in the Knuth--Morris--Pratt (KMP) algorithm. A trajectory is processed together with the corresponding data structure, allowing the proposed algorithms to be represented as transition systems whose states are trajectories for sequences and trajectories associated with nodes for partially ordered structures. The trajectory-based approach provides a unified framework for fuzzy pattern matching in various data structures.

</details>


### [217] [Improved Low-Overhead Communication-Efficient String Reconciliation and Edit Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25179)
*Michael T. Goodrich,Gonzalo Navarro,Claire A. To*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于字符串和解和编辑距离问题的低开销、通信高效的算法，能够在字符串相似时使用较少的通信量确定编辑距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决两方持有长字符串时，如何高效地确定字符串相似度并以与不相似度成比例的成本交换字符串的问题，适用于数据库同步、文件系统同步及DNA序列比较等场景。

Method: 提供了一种简单且低开销的通信高效算法，用于字符串和解及编辑距离计算。

Result: 算法仅使用$O(k\log^3 n)$比特的通信量和$O(n\log k)$的时间开销，以高概率确定字符串$X$和$Y$之间的编辑距离$k$。

Conclusion: 该算法在字符串足够相似时，实现了通信效率和计算低开销，适用于长字符串的和解与编辑距离问题。

Abstract: Suppose two parties, Alice and Bob, hold long character strings, $X$ and $Y$, respectively, and they are interested in determining how similar $X$ and $Y$ are. {Moreover, they want to exchange the strings with cost proportional to their degree of dissimilarity.} Such problems arise, for example, in database and file system synchronization operations, as well as in DNA sequence comparisons. Since the strings are long, we are interested in methods that are communication-efficient and have low overhead in terms of the computations that Alice and Bob must perform, when the strings are similar enough. In this paper, we provide a simple low-overhead communication-efficient algorithms for such string reconciliation and edit distance problems, determining the edit distance $k$ between $X$ and $Y$ using only $O(k\log^3 n)$ bits of communication and $O(n\log k)$ time overhead, with high probability.

</details>


### [218] [Exact algorithms for optimal discretization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25197)
*László Kozma,Junqi Tan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了最优离散化问题和点分离问题的新算法，分别将时间复杂度从平凡的$2^n$界限改进到$\mathcal{O}(1.9602^n)$和$\mathcal{O}(1.8906^n)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优离散化问题在监督机器学习的预处理中很重要，且在参数化算法领域受到关注。之前的研究给出了固定参数算法，但本文旨在突破平凡的时间复杂度界限。

Method: 通过对双色点集和单色点集的结构观察，设计了新的算法来解决最优离散化问题和相关的点分离问题。

Result: 最优离散化问题的算法时间复杂度为$\mathcal{O}(1.9602^n)$，点分离问题的算法时间复杂度为$\mathcal{O}(1.8906^n)$，均优于平凡的$2^n$界限。

Conclusion: 这是首次对这两个问题的平凡时间复杂度界限进行改进，且算法在点允许共享坐标的情况下仍然有效。

Abstract: The optimal discretization problem asks, given two disjoint sets of points $R$ and $B$ in the plane, for a minimal family of horizontal and vertical lines that separate the two sets, so that no cell delimited by the lines contains points from both sets. The problem arises as a pre-processing in supervised machine learning, and has received significant attention in parameterized algorithmics. Answering the question raised by Bonnet, Giannopoulos, and Lampis [IPEC 2017] and Froese [PhD thesis, 2018], it was shown by Kratsch, Masařík, Muzi, Pilipczuk, and Sorge [SODA 2021] that optimal discretization admits a fixed-parameter algorithm with running time $2^{\mathcal{O}(k^2 \log k)} \cdot n^{\mathcal{O}(1)}$, where $k$ is the solution size and $n = |R| + |B|$.
  In this paper we give an algorithm for optimal discretization that runs in time $\mathcal{O}(1.9602^n)$. We also study the related point separation problem that asks to separate all input points by axis-parallel lines. For this problem we obtain an algorithm with runtime $\mathcal{O}(1.8906^n)$. Our guarantees follow from structural observations about bichromatic and monochromatic point sets, and hold even if points are allowed to share coordinates. To our knowledge, these are the first improvements over the trivial $2^n$ bound for both problems.

</details>


### [219] [Time-Optimal APSP and Matrix Multiplication in Classes of Linear Neighborhood Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25212)
*Édouard Bonnet,Julien Duron,Marcin Pilipczuk,Marek Sokołowski,Szymon Toruńczyk*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对线性邻域复杂度图类的O(n²)时间最优算法，解决了全对最短路径和邻接矩阵乘法问题，并扩展到有界VC密度类，同时给出了三角形、K₄和K₅检测的高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性邻域复杂度是一个广泛的图类结构假设，涵盖大多数稀疏图类（如平面图、排除固定子式图、有界扩张图）和许多稠密图类（如有限团宽、孪生宽、合并宽、翻转宽图）。现有研究在这些图类上的算法效率仍有提升空间，需要更通用的最优算法框架。

Method: 通过二次预处理后，实现邻接矩阵M与任意n×n矩阵的乘法，以及与任意n向量的O(n)时间乘法。采用随机化和确定性算法设计，针对三角形、K₄、K₅检测问题分别设计不同时间复杂度的算法。

Result: 实现了全对最短路径和邻接矩阵乘法的O(n²)时间最优算法；三角形检测达到随机化线性时间O(n+m)；K₄检测为随机化O(n log⁵n + m log n)或确定性O(n²)；K₅检测为随机化O(n log⁹n + m log⁵n)。

Conclusion: 该研究解决了Bonnet等人提出的多个问题，改进并泛化了多篇近期论文的结果，将算法适用范围扩展到线性邻域复杂度类及有界VC密度类，为图算法设计提供了更高效的通用框架。

Abstract: The notion of linear neighborhood complexity is a very general structural assumption on a graph class, covering most classes of sparse graphs such as planar graphs, graphs excluding a fixed (topological) minor, or bounded expansion graphs, as well as many structured classes of dense graphs, such as graphs of bounded clique-width, twin-width, merge-width, or flip-width. In this work, we present $O(n^2)$-time optimal algorithms for $n$-vertex graphs coming from a class of linear neighborhood complexity for the following problems: $\bullet$ All-Pairs Shortest Paths, $\bullet$ the multiplication of the adjacency matrix $M$ of the input graph with any $n \times n$ matrix. More specifically, after a quadratic preprocessing, we can multiply $M$ with any $n$-vector in $O(n)$ time.
  This solves several questions raised in [Bonnet, Kim, Geniet, Moon; ICALP '26], and improves and generalizes results in several other recent papers [Bonnet, Giocanti, Ossona de Mendez, Thomassé; STACS '23], [Bannach, Marwitz, Tantau; STACS '24], [Anand, van den Brand, McCarty; NeurIPS '26], [Kozma, Opler '26], and [Cardinal, McCarty, Yuditsky '26]. We also extend our results to classes of bounded VC density. In classes of linear neighborhood complexity, we also give a triangle-detection algorithm in randomized linear time $O(n+m)$ in $n$-vertex $m$-edge graphs, a $K_4$-detection algorithm in randomized $O(n \log^5 n + m \log n)$ or deterministic $O(n^2)$ time, and a $K_5$-detection algorithm in randomized $O(n \log^9 n + m \log^5 n)$ time.

</details>


### [220] [A General Framework for Metropolis-Adjusted Dikin Walks: Dimension-Square Mixing on Polytopes and Log-Det Walks on Spectrahedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25273)
*Zhao Song,Lichen Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文分析精确度量、Metropolis 调整的 Dikin 行走，通过保持提议行列式和反向二次型在一起，实现了多面体和谱面体的快速混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何通过改进 Dikin 行走的接受率分析来提高马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛方法的混合速度，特别是在处理多面体和谱面体时。

Method: 通过将提议行列式和反向二次型结合分析，消除主导未中心化项，利用二阶工具控制中心化波动，并采用接受率到混合率的归约方法。

Result: 对于由 n 个不等式给出的多面体和凸 L-Lipschitz 势，正则化 Lee-Sidford 行走的预热混合步数为 $\widetilde O((d^{2}+dL^{2}R^{2})\log(w/δ))$；对于具有 $n\times n$ 块的谱面体，log-det 行走的混合步数为 $\widetilde O((ψ^\star nd+dL^{2}R^{2})\log(w/δ))$。

Conclusion: 通过提议比较论证将多面体界限转移到高精度 Lewis 权重计算的度量，并为谱面体提供基于 TensorSRHT 构造的精确算术实现，实现了高效的混合率。

Abstract: We analyze exact-metric, Metropolis-adjusted Dikin walks by keeping the proposal determinant and reverse quadratic form together. Their leading uncentered terms cancel in the complete logarithmic acceptance ratio, leaving centered fluctuations that can be controlled with second-order tools. For a polytope given by $n$ inequalities and a convex $L$-Lipschitz potential, this yields warm-start mixing in $\widetilde O((d^{2}+dL^{2}R^{2})\log(w/δ))$ steps for the regularized Lee--Sidford walk. For a spectrahedron with $n\times n$ blocks, the log-det walk mixes in $\widetilde O((ψ^\star nd+dL^{2}R^{2})\log(w/δ))$ steps, where $ψ^\star$ measures matrix leverage.
  The two analyses share an acceptance-to-mixing reduction. A proposal-comparison argument transfers the polytope bound to an appropriately padded $O(1/d)$-accurate metric computed from high-precision Lewis weights. For spectrahedra, given $\widehatψ\geψ^\star$, a direct-or-two-seed TensorSRHT construction gives an exact-arithmetic implementation with $ψ^\star$ replaced by $\widehatψ$ in the mixing bound.

</details>


### [221] [Paging with Per-Replacement Maximum Delay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25290)
*Tianhang Lu,Runtian Ren,Shengcai Liu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了允许页面缺失延迟处理的“每替换最大延迟”模型，证明了经典分页的竞争性层次结构在该模型下依然成立，并提出了相应的确定性及随机化算法，同时揭示了离线场景下经典最远下次使用策略的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当页面缺失不必立即触发替换（即允许等待）时，经典分页算法的结构和性质会发生何种变化，特别是在引入基于页面年龄的延迟成本模型后。

Method: 提出了一种“每替换最大延迟”模型，其中加载待处理页面的成本包含移动单元及其最旧请求的年龄。设计了确定性阈值-LRU算法和基于缓存无关时间窗口的随机化算法（结合影子分页投影技术）。在离线方面，开发了精确动态规划算法及多项式时间的近似算法。

Result: 对于大小为$k$的缓存，实现了$(5k+3)$-竞争的确定性算法和$5H_k$-竞争的随机化算法，其阶数与经典下界匹配。离线方面给出了$O(nk)$精确动态规划及5-近似算法，但发现“最远下次使用”策略在物理延迟问题中即使只有三个页面也可能次优。

Conclusion: 经典分页的竞争性层次结构在允许延迟的模型中得以保留，但离线最优策略的性质发生了根本变化，经典的贪心策略（如最远下次使用）不再保证最优。

Abstract: Classical paging couples every miss to an immediate replacement. We ask what remains of its algorithmic structure when a miss may wait. In our per-replacement maximum-delay model, loading a pending page costs one unit of movement plus the age of its oldest outstanding request and clears the whole page-specific episode. Equivalently, the instantaneous holding rate is the number of pending pages, rather than the number of pending requests.
  The classical competitive hierarchy survives this change. For cache size $k$, we give a deterministic $(5k+3)$-competitive threshold-LRU algorithm and a randomized $5H_k$-competitive algorithm against an oblivious adversary; classical lower-bound instances give matching $Ω(k)$ and $Ω(H_k)$ orders. The randomized algorithm uses cache-independent temporal windows to create an ordinary-paging sequence fixed before any random choices; a shadow paging algorithm is then projected onto nonproactive physical replacements.
  The offline picture is less classical. We give an exact $O(nk)$ dynamic program with one hole, an exact configuration dynamic program for a fixed number of holes, and a deterministic nonproactive polynomial-time $5$-approximation without fixing that number. Yet farthest-next-use victim selection can be suboptimal in the physical delayed problem already with three pages.

</details>


### [222] [A Spectral Local-to-Global Principle for Spin Systems on Graphs with Girth At Least Five](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25491)
*Xiaoyu Chen,Kuikui Liu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了在围长至少为5且最大度$\Delta$足够大的图上，当颜色数$q \geq (1+\delta)\Delta$时，均匀分布的$q$-着色Glauber动力学是快速混合的；该结论通过新的谱局部到全局原理及星图上的傅里叶分析，推广至满足局部谱收缩条件的多自旋系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高围长图上多自旋系统（如图着色和反铁磁Potts模型）Glauber动力学混合时间的临界阈值问题，特别是突破传统界限，证明在$q$接近最大度$\Delta$时的快速混合性。

Method: 提出了针对围长至少为5的图的通用多自旋系统“谱局部到全局原理”（spectral local-to-global principle），并开发了针对星图（star）上Glauber动力学的新型傅里叶分析方法。

Result: 确立了当$q \geq (1+\delta)\Delta$且图围长$\geq 5$时，均匀$q$-着色动力学的快速混合性；并将结果推广至满足局部谱收缩条件的系统，如$q \geq (1+\delta)(1-\beta)\Delta$的反铁磁Potts模型。

Conclusion: 通过结合新的谱理论工具与傅里叶分析技术，成功建立了高围长图上多自旋系统快速混合的充分条件，显著推进了对稀疏图结构下马尔可夫链收敛行为的理论理解。

Abstract: It is proved that, for every $δ\in (0,1)$, the Glauber dynamics for the uniform distribution on proper $q$-colorings is rapidly mixing when $q \geq (1+δ)Δ$ and the underlying graph has girth at least $5$ and maximum degree $Δ= Ω_δ(1)$. This result also extends to general multi-spin systems satisfying a $\textit{local spectral contraction}$ condition, including the anti-ferromagnetic Potts model with $q\geq (1+δ)(1-β)Δ$. These results are achieved by a new spectral local-to-global principle on graphs with girth at least five for general multi-spin systems, and a novel Fourier analysis for Glauber dynamics on a star. The main ideas behind all the proofs were developed through several rounds of interaction with GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra.

</details>


### [223] [Not All Degree Constraints Are Created Equal when Computing Spanning Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25530)
*Narek Bojikian,Alexander Firbas,Robert Ganian,Hung P. Hoang,Krisztina Szilagyi*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了局部度约束下的最小生成树问题，发现在有界树深（treedepth）参数化下，三种变体的复杂性出现根本分歧：指定度数和有界度数问题是固定参数可解的（FPT），而允许度数集合问题则是 W[1]-难的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管近期研究指出三种度约束最小生成树问题在树宽、路径宽等标准结构参数下具有相同的参数化复杂性，但本文旨在探究在更严格的参数化（特别是有界树深）下，这些问题的复杂性景观是否依然一致。

Method: 通过参数化复杂性分析，针对有界树深图（bounded-treedepth graphs）分别证明“指定度数 MST”和“有界度数 MST”的固定参数可解性（FPT），并证明“允许度数集合 MST”在树深及结合反馈顶点集参数下的 W[1]-难性；同时分析了顶点覆盖数和删除距离至常数路径宽等其他参数下的复杂性边界。

Result: 1. 指定度数 MST 和有界度数 MST 在树深参数化下是 FPT 的。2. 允许度数集合 MST 在树深参数化下是 W[1]-难的，即使结合反馈顶点数也是如此。3. 这种复杂性分歧特定于树深：允许度数集合 MST 关于顶点覆盖数未表现出类似的 W[1]-难性，而前两个问题关于删除距离至常数路径宽不存在 FPT 算法。

Conclusion: 在有界树深图上，三种自然形式化的度约束最小生成树问题的复杂性景观发生根本性分离，表明树深是揭示这些细微复杂性差异的关键结构参数，而其他参数（如顶点覆盖数）则无法捕捉到这种分歧。

Abstract: We study the computation of minimum spanning trees subject to local degree constraints. Recent work (ICALP 2026) established that three natural formalizations of this problem share the exact same parameterized complexity under standard structural graph parameters, including treewidth, pathwidth and clique-width. This applies to the cases where every vertex has a single target degree (Specified Degree MST), or a degree upper bound (Bounded Degree MST), or is equipped with a set of admissible degrees (Set of Degrees MST).
  In this paper, we investigate these problems under more restrictive parameterizations and reveal that their complexity landscapes fundamentally diverge on bounded-treedepth graphs. Specifically, we prove that the former two problems are fixed-parameter tractable when parameterized by the treedepth of the input graph. In sharp contrast, we show that Set of Degrees MST remains W[1]-hard parameterized by treedepth, even when combined with the feedback vertex number (i.e., deletion distance to treewidth $1$). Finally, we show that this divergence seems to be specific to treedepth: we exclude an analogous W[1]-hardness result for Set of Degrees MST w.r.t. the vertex cover number and also rule out fixed-parameter algorithms for the former two problems w.r.t. deletion distance to constant pathwidth.

</details>


### [224] [Partially-Dynamic All-Pairs Maxflow and Effective Resistance via Stable Sparsifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26037)
*Gramoz Goranci,Rasmus Kyng,Maximilian Probst Gutenberg,Yibin Zhao,Gernot Zöcklein*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对无向加权图的部分动态随机数据结构，能在仅插入或仅删除边的情况下，以$\widetilde{O}_ε(n^2)$的总更新时间和$\widetilde{O}_ε(1)$的最坏情况查询时间，维护任意顶点对之间最大流值和有效电阻的$(1\pmε)$近似值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决稠密图（$m = Ω(n^2)$）在部分动态（仅插入或仅删除）场景下，高效维护最大流和有效电阻近似值的挑战，旨在实现接近最优的算法保证，并能抵抗自适应 adversaries。

Method: 基于部分动态图的稳定性原则，将在线更新序列划分为$\widetilde{O}(n/ε)$个时期（epochs）。利用累积杠杆分数（leverage score）确定时期边界：小的杠杆分数质量意味着小的谱变化，而单调更新序列的总杠杆分数质量为$\widetilde{O}(n)$。因此，只需每个时期重新计算一次谱稀疏化器（spectral sparsifier），并对这些稀疏化器应用已知的静态全点对最大流和有效电阻预言机。

Result: 实现了总更新时间$\widetilde{O}_ε(n^2)$和最坏情况查询时间$\widetilde{O}_ε(1)$，对于稠密图而言，该保证接近最优。算法以高概率成功抵抗自适应 adversaries。

Conclusion: 通过利用杠杆分数控制的谱稳定性将动态更新划分为稀疏重计算时期，结合静态预言机，成功构建了高效的部分动态数据结构，解决了稠密图中最大流和有效电阻的动态维护问题。

Abstract: We give a randomized data structure for undirected weighted graphs that are partially dynamic, i.e., that undergo either only edge insertions or only edge deletions. The data structure maintains $(1\pmε)$-approximations to the maxflow value and effective resistance between any queried pair of vertices, with total update time $\widetilde{O}_ε(n^2)$ and worst-case query time $\widetilde{O}_ε(1)$. Thus, for dense graphs where $m = Ω(n^2)$, our guarantees are near-optimal. Our algorithms succeed with high probability against an adaptive adversary.
  Our result follows from a simple stability principle for partially dynamic graphs. We show how to partition an online sequence of $m$ updates into $\widetilde{O}(n/ε)$ epochs such that every graph within an epoch is a $(1\pm O(ε))$-spectral approximation of the graph at the beginning of the epoch. The epochs are determined by the cumulative leverage score of the updated edges: small leverage-score mass implies small spectral change, while the total leverage-score mass over a monotone update sequence is $\widetilde{O}(n)$. Consequently, a spectral sparsifier needs to be recomputed only once per epoch. Applying known static all-pairs maxflow and effective-resistance oracles to these sparsifiers then yields the result.

</details>


### [225] [Nearly Optimal Strong Coresets for $\ell_p$ Subspace Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26047)
*Honghao Lin,Vahab Mirrokni,David P. Woodruff*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文改进了$\ell_p$子空间逼近的强核心集（strong coresets）大小界限，将$1\leq p<2$和$p>2$两种情况下的$\eps$依赖度均优化至$\eps^{-2}$，并在运行时间上达到了最优或匹配现有最佳框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Woodruff和Yasuda (FOCS 2025) 提出的核心集大小在$\eps$的依赖度上分别为$\eps^{-4/p}$ ($1\leq p<2$) 和$\eps^{-p}$ ($p>2$)，尚未达到理论下界。本文旨在缩小这一差距，提供更紧致的核心集大小界限，并匹配已知的采样下界。

Method: 针对$1\leq p<2$，结合双标准低秩分裂（bicriteria low-rank split）、Lewis权重采样以及与输出维度无关的经验过程界；针对$p>2$，对Woodruff-Yasuda构造进行更精细的分析，通过在行计数递归中保留采样概率的截断项，证明了其可达到$\eps^{-2}$的依赖度。

Result: 将核心集大小改进为$\wtO_p(k\eps^{-2})$ ($1\leq p<2$) 和$\wtO_p(k^{p/2}\eps^{-2})$ ($p>2$)。对于$1\leq p<2$，算法运行时间为$\wtO_p(\nnz(\A)+d^ω+k\eps^{-2})$；对于$p>2$，运行时间为$\wtO_p(\nnz(\A)+d^ω)$，与Woodruff-Yasuda框架持平。

Conclusion: 该研究显著提升了$\ell_p$子空间逼近核心集的理论界限，特别是在误差参数$\eps$的依赖度上达到了最优的平方反比关系，且在特定条件下匹配了采样下界，同时保持了高效的计算复杂度。

Abstract: We study strong coresets for $\ell_p$ subspace approximation. Given $\A\in\R^{n\times d}$, the goal is to sample and rescale a small number of its rows to form $\Smat\A$ such that \[
  \norm{\Smat\A(\I-\Pmat_F)}_{p,2}^p
  =(1\pm\eps)\norm{\A(\I-\Pmat_F)}_{p,2}^p \] simultaneously for every subspace $F\subseteq\R^d$ of dimension at most $k$, where $\Pmat_F$ is the orthogonal projector onto $F$. Woodruff and Yasuda (FOCS 2025)~\cite{WY25} obtained coreset sizes $\wtO_p(k\eps^{-4/p})$ for $1\leq p<2$ and $\wtO_p(k^{p/2}\eps^{-p})$ for $p>2$. We improve these bounds to $\wtO_p(k\eps^{-2})$ and $\wtO_p(k^{p/2}\eps^{-2})$, respectively. For $1\leq p<2$, our algorithm runs in $\wtO_p(\nnz(\A)+d^ω+k\eps^{-2})$ time. The resulting coreset size matches the known sampling lower bound~\cite{LWW21} up to logarithmic factors when $k+1\geq C\log(1/\eps)$ for an absolute constant $C$. For $p>2$, our algorithm runs in $\wtO_p(\nnz(\A)+d^ω)$ time, matching the running time of the Woodruff--Yasuda framework.
  We use different techniques in the two regimes. For $1\leq p<2$, we combine a bicriteria low-rank split with Lewis-weight sampling and empirical-process bounds independent of the output dimension. For $p>2$, we give a sharper analysis of the Woodruff--Yasuda construction. By retaining the truncation in its sampling probabilities throughout the row-count recurrence, we show that it achieves the improved $\eps^{-2}$ dependence.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [226] [Common-Center Geometry and Certified Radial Reconstruction for Energy-Form Full Conformal Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24964)
*Yiheng Feng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于经验能量形成对分数的全共形预测（FullCP）区域的几何性质，证明了在特定条件下这些区域是星形的而非一定是凸的，并针对幂距离提出了具体的几何边界和重构方法，主要适用于低维多变量输出场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决候选分数凸性无法保证全共形预测区域连通性的问题，特别是当分数为经验平均且损失函数在第一参数上凸时。旨在通过深入分析能量形式分数的几何结构，为低维多变量输出提供精确的预测区域重构和理论保证。

Method: 通过直接展开留一法（leave-one-out）分数，将能量形式分数的每个训练点比较转化为成对不相似性子水平条件。在对称性、常数对角线、对角线下界及达到相关Fréchet型目标的假设下，分析比较区域的几何特性。针对幂距离 $ρ_β(x,y)=\|x-y\|^β$，利用特定于分数的边界、方向根搜索思想及经典Lipschitz扩展机制，推导认证的内部和外部径向包络。

Result: 1. 在满足特定条件下，非平凡精确共形区域关于共同最小化点呈星形。2. 对于幂距离，当 $β\ge1$ 时确定性几何性质成立，而传统能量分数在 $0<β<2$ 时严格适当。3. 在一维 $β=1$ 情况下，非平凡经验CRPS FullCP区域为非空闭区间。4. 在 $1<β<2$ 且 $m\ge2$ 时，导数边界提供了Lipschitz控制，从而得到宽度受限的径向包络及相应的Hausdorff保证。5. 二维解析示例表明保留星形但非凸几何结构的重要性。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种基于重构的视角，明确了全共形预测区域的星形几何特征及其边界控制方法。该方法旨在服务于低维多变量输出的精确预测区域构建，而非追求高维扩展性或运行时间的优化。

Abstract: This note studies the geometry of full conformal prediction (FullCP) regions generated by an empirical energy-form pairwise score. Candidate-score convexity alone does not guarantee connected FullCP regions, even when the candidate score is an empirical average of a loss convex in its first argument. Direct expansion of the leave-one-out scores shows that each training-point comparison for the energy-form score is exactly a pairwise-dissimilarity sublevel condition. Under symmetry, a constant diagonal, a diagonal lower bound, and attainment of the associated Fréchet-type objective, every comparison region contains a common minimizer; when the comparison regions are convex, the nontrivial exact conformal region is therefore star-shaped about that same point. For power distances $ρ_β(x,y)=\|x-y\|^β$, this deterministic geometry holds for $β\ge1$, while the conventional energy score is strictly proper for $0<β<2$. In the univariate $β=1$ specialization, every nontrivial empirical-CRPS FullCP region is a nonempty closed interval, possibly $\mathbb R$ in the $m=1$ degeneracy. On the unconditional reconstruction range $1<β<2$ and $m\ge2$, explicit data-checkable derivative bounds yield Lipschitz control of the comparison-set radial exits and hence of the exact conformal radial function. These score-specific bounds permit existing directional root-search ideas and classical Lipschitz-extension machinery to yield certified inner and outer radial envelopes with width at most $δ+2Lh_{\mathcal U}$ and corresponding same-ray Hausdorff guarantees. An analytic two-dimensional example shows why retaining star-shaped but nonconvex geometry can matter. The resulting reconstruction perspective is intended for low-dimensional multivariate outputs rather than high-dimensional scaling or runtime improvement.

</details>


### [227] [Improved Analysis for Hessian-free High-resolution Monte Carlo Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25052)
*Wujun Lv,Xiaoyu Wang,Yingli Wang,Lingjiong Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了Hessian-free high-resolution (HFHR) 动力学，通过为欠阻尼朗之万动力学(ULD) 增加可逆位置扩散，解决了机器学习中的采样问题，并证明了其优于现有算法的收敛速率和迭代复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器学习采样问题中，现有欠阻尼朗之万动力学(ULD) 在收敛速率和迭代复杂度方面的局限性，特别是在势函数非凸的情况下。

Method: 建立HFHR动力学的显式定量收缩率，利用位置Poincaré不等式、加权Hessian和Laplacian界及紧Sobolev嵌入；采用路径空间Girsanov论证获得HFHRMC算法的非渐近收敛界和迭代复杂度。

Result: 获得了优于ULD的显式收缩率，推导出HFHRMC算法的总变差距离非渐近收敛界和迭代复杂度，且边界在所有参数下保持正则；数值实验验证了正位置扩散参数的有效性。

Conclusion: HFHR动力学及其离散化算法HFHRMC在非凸势函数采样问题上具有理论优势，优化后的迭代复杂度优于现有HFHR算法，且在高精度下与优化的ULD端点一致。

Abstract: Hessian-free high-resolution (HFHR) dynamics augments underdamped Langevin dynamics (ULD) with reversible position diffusion for sampling problems that arise in machine learning. We establish an explicit quantitative contraction rate for HFHR dynamics under a position Poincaré inequality, weighted Hessian and Laplacian bounds, and a compact Sobolev embedding, where the potential function is not necessarily convex. An adapted time-augmented Poincaré inequality yields an explicit rate that improves upon the contraction rate of the underdamped Langevin dynamics. We also give a weak-solution construction and a self-contained spectral proof of the divergence lemma underlying the argument. For HFHR Monte Carlo (HFHRMC) algorithm, which is based on a discretization scheme of HFHR dynamics, we use a path-space Girsanov argument to obtain a non-asymptotic convergence bound and an explicit iteration complexity in total variation distance. The bounds hold for every $α\geq0$ and $γ>0$ and remain regular at the ULD endpoint. Optimizing the iteration complexity bound yields a positive, accuracy-dependent position-diffusion parameter at finite accuracy, while its leading high-accuracy order coincides with that of the optimized ULD endpoint. Our iteration complexity bound improves upon the existing work on HFHR algorithms. Numerical experiments including Bayesian learning problems on real data are provided to illustrate the effect of positive $α$ and its benefit.

</details>


### [228] [Minimax Alternating Regret for the Experts Problem and Online Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25182)
*Mengxiao Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文解决了在线凸优化（OCO）中交替遗憾（alternating regret）的极小极大遗憾率这一开放问题。作者证明了对于$d$专家问题，极小极大交替遗憾为$Θ(\log d)$，与时间范围$T$无关；对于一般$d$维OCO问题，该值为$Θ(d\log(1+T/d))$。这两项结果均显著优于现有界限，并通过修正的Hedge算法及多尺度构造分别实现了最优上界与下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受双人博弈中交替学习动态成功的启发，研究OCO中的交替遗憾。尽管先前研究在各种假设下实现了$o(\sqrt{T})$的交替遗憾，但即使是专家问题，其极小极大遗憾率仍未解决。

Method: 上界方面：针对专家问题提出一种修正变体的Hedge算法，通过精心设计的修正项抵消交替遗憾分析中产生的不利曲率；将此修正势函数论证扩展至连续动作集以处理一般OCO。下界方面：专家问题构造通过反复消除一半候选专家实现；OCO下界则通过在单位圆盘上构建更复杂的多尺度构造来替代离散消除过程。

Result: 1. $d$专家问题的极小极大交替遗憾为$Θ(\log d)$，独立于$T$，优于Hait等人[2025]的$\mathcal{O}(T^{1/3}\log^{2/3} d)$。
2. $d$维紧凸集上的一般OCO问题，最坏情况极小极大交替遗憾为$Θ(d\log(1+T/d))$，优于Cevher等人[2023]及Hait等人[2025]提出的$\mathcal{O}((d\log T)^{2/3}T^{1/3})$上界。
3. 建立了匹配的下界与上界，彻底解决了该领域的开放问题。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入修正势函数方法和创新的下界构造技术，确立了专家问题及一般OCO问题中交替遗憾的精确极小极大速率，显著提升了该领域的理论认知并解决了长期存在的开放问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we study alternating regret in online convex optimization (OCO), motivated by the success of alternating learning dynamics in two-player games. Although previous works have shown that $o(\sqrt{T})$ alternating regret is achievable under various assumptions on the loss functions and feasible domains, the minimax regret rate has remained open even for the expert problem. In this paper, we resolve this question by showing matching lower and upper bounds for both the expert problem and general OCO. Somewhat surprisingly, for the $d$-expert problem, we show that the minimax alternating regret is $Θ(\log d)$, independent of the horizon $T$. This significantly improves upon the best-known $\mathcal{O}(T^{1/3}\log^{2/3} d)$ established by Hait et al. [2025]. We further extend our results to general OCO over a $d$-dimensional compact convex set and prove that the worst-case minimax alternating regret is $Θ\left(d\log \left(1+\frac{T}{d}\right)\right)$, also significantly improving upon the best-known $\mathcal{O}((d\log T)^{2/3}T^{1/3})$ upper bound and resolving the open problem posed by Cevher et al. [2023], Hait et al. [2025]. Technically, our upper bound for the expert problem is achieved by a corrected variant of Hedge, in which carefully designed correction terms cancel the unfavorable curvature arising in the alternating-regret analysis. We extend the same corrected-potential argument to continuous action sets to obtain the optimal alternating-regret rate for OCO. For the lower bounds, the expert construction repeatedly eliminates half of the candidate experts, while the OCO lower bound instance construction replaces this discrete elimination by a more involved multiscale construction on the unit disk.

</details>


### [229] [Adaptive Regularization for Random Features: A Neighboring Early-Stopping Rule with Oracle-Rate Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25513)
*Caixing Wang,Zhibo Chen,Yue Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对随机特征核岭回归（KRR-RF）的自适应正则化方法，通过邻域早停规则在无需先验知识的情况下选择正则化参数，达到接近最优的学习率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机特征方法虽可扩展核岭回归，但其最优正则化参数依赖于未知的平滑度和容量参数，现有方法缺乏自适应选择机制。

Method: 提出邻域早停规则，使用逆正则化均匀网格并仅比较相邻估计器，直接在随机特征空间计算邻域差异和经验复杂度项，避免构建精确核Gram矩阵。

Result: 建立了邻域KRR-RF估计器的高概率比较界，证明在标准源和容量条件下，所选估计器可达Oracle多项式学习率（含对数因子），适用于设定正确和部分错误设定情形。

Conclusion: 该方法无需先验知识即可选择正则化参数，通过经验随机特征有效维度连接可观测停止阈值与模型总体复杂度，实验验证了其预测性能和计算效率优于标准调参方法。

Abstract: Random feature methods provide a scalable approximation to kernel ridge regression (KRR), but the regularization parameter that yields the oracle learning rate depends on unknown smoothness and capacity parameters. In this work, we propose a neighboring early-stopping rule for adaptive regularization in KRR with random features (KRR-RF). The method uses a grid that is uniform in inverse regularization and compares only adjacent estimators, reducing the number of discrepancy comparisons relative to standard all-pairs Lepskii-type procedures. Both the neighboring discrepancy and its empirical complexity term can be computed directly in the random feature space, without constructing the exact kernel Gram matrix.
  We establish a high-probability comparison bound for neighboring KRR-RF estimators and show that, under standard source and capacity conditions together with suitable grid and random feature budget conditions, the selected estimator attains the oracle polynomial learning rate up to logarithmic factors. The result allows the regularization parameter to be selected without prior knowledge of the source and capacity exponents and covers both well-specified and partially misspecified regimes. Our analysis is based on an empirical random feature effective dimension that connects the observable stopping threshold with the population complexity of the random feature model. Simulation and real-data experiments illustrate the prediction performance and computational behavior of the proposed method in comparison with standard tuning procedures.

</details>


### [230] [Fast rates in Bayesian online learning with approximate posteriors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25706)
*Ilsang Ohn*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了只要近似后验分布能足够精确地追踪精确吉布斯后验（通过Wasserstein距离衡量），近似贝叶斯预测即可继承精确贝叶斯预测的快速遗憾界，并在三个在线学习场景中验证了该理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确贝叶斯预测虽具备快速预测遗憾保证，但其后验更新或表示的计算成本过高，难以应用于在线场景；因此需要研究计算近似方法在何种条件下能保留这些统计保证。

Method: 提出一个通用定理，将后验近似的累积代价表示为精确吉布斯后验的收缩半径与近似/精确后验间Wasserstein距离的相互作用；并通过三个具体在线学习实例（线性模型、无限维指数族序列模型、高斯过程回归）进行实证分析。

Result: 1. 线性模型中，投影朗之万算法实现的近似后验获得了对数遗憾界；2. 无限维模型中，保先验截断法以次线性内存和常数更新成本达到了极小极大预测遗憾率；3. 高斯过程回归中，稀疏变分后验在显著降低计算成本的同时，达到了与精确高斯过程相同的预测遗憾率。

Conclusion: 任何能以足够精度追踪精确后验的近似后验方法，均可继承精确贝叶斯预测的快速遗憾界，其性能损失仅由近似误差决定的加性项控制，从而为高效在线贝叶斯学习提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Exact Bayes prediction enjoys fast predictive regret guarantees, but exact posterior updating or representation may be too costly for online use. We study when these statistical guarantees are preserved by computational approximations. We show that the cumulative price of posterior approximation can be governed by the interaction between the contraction radius of the exact Gibbs posterior and the Wasserstein distance between the approximate and exact posteriors. Our general theorem shows that whenever exact Bayes prediction achieves a fast regret bound, any approximate posterior method that tracks the exact posterior with sufficient accuracy inherits the same fast regret, up to an additive term determined by the approximation error. Three online learning examples are developed. For linear models with strongly convex regularized losses, a projected Langevin algorithm yields an approximate posterior that achieves logarithmic regret. For an infinite-dimensional canonical exponential family sequence model over a Sobolev ellipsoid, a prior-preserving truncation method attains the minimax predictive regret rate with sublinear memory and constant update cost per observation. For random-design Gaussian process (GP) regression, a sparse variational posterior with inducing variables achieves the same predictive regret rate as the exact GP, but at substantially lower computational cost.

</details>


### [231] [Multi-output Gaussian process prediction of physical fields under linear equality constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25709)
*Mahamat Hamdan Nassouradine,Clément Gauchy,Pierre-Emmanuel Angeli,Sébastien da Veiga*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于行式PCA和线性约束多输出高斯过程的框架，用于在保持物理约束的同时联合预测多个高维物理场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物理机器学习中，同时预测受线性等式约束的多个高维物理场时，传统高斯过程回归面临两大挑战：输出场的高维度和物理约束的强制执行。现有通过约束关系推导某一输出的方法对选择哪个输出敏感，影响预测精度和不确定性量化，需要一种对称处理所有场且严格尊重物理约束的方法。

Method: 首先采用一种名为"行式PCA"的特定主成分分析程序处理多场数据，该方法能在潜在空间中保留约束；然后基于特定的核参数化，在行式PCA的潜在空间上训练线性约束多输出高斯过程模型。

Result: 该框架在种群动力学问题和工业CFD应用（涉及不可压缩约束下的雷诺应力张量分量预测）上进行了验证，证明了其有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的框架能够对称处理所有物理场并严格保持底层物理约束，解决了传统方法中因任意选择推导输出而导致的预测精度和不确定性量化问题，为受约束多场数据的联合建模提供了稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: We address the simultaneous prediction of multiple high-dimensional physical fields governed by linear equality constraints, a setting that arises in many real-world applications in physics machine learning. Gaussian process (GP) regression is a widely used surrogate modeling approach due to its effectiveness in small-sample regimes and its ability to provide uncertainty quantification. However, applying GP models in this setting raises two major challenges: the high dimensionality of the discretized output fields and the enforcement of the physical constraint in predictions. For the latter, a common strategy consists in deducing one output from the others via the constraint relation. Through a benchmark, we show that this deductive approach is sensitive to the arbitrary choice of which output to deduce, affecting both predictive accuracy and uncertainty quantification. Consequently, there is a need for an approach that treats all fields symmetrically while strictly respecting the underlying physics. Motivated by these limitations, we propose a robust framework for jointly modeling constrained multi-field data. Our approach first leverages a specific PCA procedure for multi-field data, coined row-wise PCA, which has the interesting property of preserving the constraint in the latent space. Since standard PCA strategies for multi-field data do not preserve such constraints, we investigate theoretically the optimality of the row-wise choice. In a second step, we consider a linearly-constrained multi-output GP approach based on a specific kernel parametrization which is trained on the latent space of row-wise PCA. The proposed framework is validated on a population dynamics problem and on an industrial CFD application, which involves the prediction of Reynolds stress tensor components under the incompressibility constraint.

</details>


### [232] [Efficient Estimation of High Information Projections using Nearest Neighbours](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25887)
*David P. Hofmeyr*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于增强数据中最近邻关系的直观降维方法，通过谱分解编码局部协方差结构的矩阵来实现，该矩阵是密度信息矩阵（DIM）的一致估计量，可用于聚类分析和异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的密度信息矩阵（DIM）估计方法计算成本高，且仅针对与真实密度平方成正比的代理密度的DIM，需要一种更高效且直接针对真实密度的降维方法。

Method: 提出一种基于谱分解的方法，通过设计一个编码数据局部协方差结构的矩阵，利用每对最近邻点捕获局部协方差，从而实现降维。

Result: 在标准正则性条件下，该方法得到的矩阵是密度信息矩阵（DIM）的一致估计量，且在实际应用中有助于聚类分析和异常检测任务。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种高效且直观的降维途径，与独立成分分析和充分降维问题相关联，并在下游任务中展现出实用价值。

Abstract: An intuitive method for dimensionality reduction is proposed, which is highly effective for finding interesting projections of multivariate data. Following similar intuitive motivation to a number of existing techniques, the proposed method is based on enhancing the nearest neighbour relationships in the data. The proposed projection arises from the spectral decomposition of a matrix designed to encode the local covariance structure in the data, where the local covariance at a point is captured by pairs of its nearest neighbours. We show that under standard regularity conditions this matrix is a consistent estimator of the so-called ``Density Information Matrix'' (DIM); a non-parametric analogue of the Fisher Information Matrix. Spectral decompositions of DIMs have been shown to be connected with the important problems of Independent Components Analysis and, in the supervised context, Sufficient Dimension Reduction. However, existing estimators of the DIM are computationally expensive to compute and only target the DIM of a surrogate density, which is proportional to the square of the true underlying density. In addition, we go on to explore the practical utility of our method in aiding the downstream tasks of cluster analysis and outlier detection.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [Modality Contribution Score - A Per-Patient Framework for Quantifying the Relative Diagnostic Contribution of Structural MRI and Amyloid PET in Alzheimer's Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24931)
*Dawa Chyophel Lepcha,Aaliya Ali,Sophie A. Martin,Deepika Koundal,Pierrick Coupe,Shabbir Syed-Abdul*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了模态贡献网络（MCNet）和模态贡献评分（MCS），这是首个针对阿尔茨海默病（AD）连续体中单个患者的多模态神经影像（MRI和PET）归因框架，能够量化从结构萎缩到淀粉样蛋白及代谢功能障碍的模态主导权转移，并在ADNI-3和OASIS-3数据集上验证了其分期性能、临床相关性及跨队列泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态神经影像人工智能系统通常仅输出单一的诊断标签，缺乏对特定患者决策驱动因素（即哪种成像模态起主导作用）的量化解释，无法满足临床对可解释性和个性化决策的需求。

Method: 引入模态贡献网络（MCNet）及基于模态消融（modality ablation）的模态贡献评分（MCS），强制每个受试者的MRI和PET贡献值归一化为1.0；在327名ADNI-3参与者（认知正常、MCI、AD组）上训练，并通过SHAP值进行机制对比，最后在1,073名OASIS-3受试者中进行外部验证。

Result: MCNet实现了具有竞争力的三分类分期性能（AUC=0.881）；MCS显示出从认知正常到AD的显著单调梯度（p<0.0001），PET主导权随病情进展显著增加，且与淀粉样蛋白SUVR及FDG代谢生物标志物显著相关；外部复制证实了跨队列泛化性；相比SHAP，基于消融的MCS能更准确地捕捉具有临床意义的模态依赖性。

Conclusion: MCNet为痴呆症护理中的个性化影像决策、临床试验分层及可信赖AI奠定了坚实基础，提供了流体生物标志物无法比拟的可解释且具临床操作性的评分体系。

Abstract: Multimodal neuroimaging combining structural MRI and positron emission tomography (PET) captures complementary structure-function relationships across the Alzheimer's disease (AD) continuum, yet existing artificial intelligence systems produce a single diagnostic label without quantifying which imaging modality drove that decision for a specific patient. We introduce the Modality Contribution Network (MCNet) and the Modality Contribution Score (MCS), the first per-patient attribution framework quantifying the shift in modality dominance from structural atrophy to amyloid and metabolic dysfunction across the cognitively normal to MCI to AD continuum. MCS is normalised to unity per subject via modality ablation (MCS_MRI_i + MCS_PET_i = 1.0 for every subject i), providing an interpretable, clinically actionable score that fluid biomarkers cannot supply. Applied to 327 ADNI-3 participants balanced across cognitively normal, mild cognitive impairment, and AD groups, MCNet achieved competitive three-class staging performance (AUC=0.881). The MCS revealed a statistically significant monotonic gradient (Kruskal-Wallis p<0.0001), with increasing PET dominance from cognitively normal (MCS_PET 0.412+/-0.229) through MCI (0.489+/-0.289) to AD (0.671+/-0.426), validated against amyloid SUVR (r=0.172, p=0.006) and FDG metabolic biomarkers (r=-0.287, p=0.0005) from separate imaging pipelines. External replication in 1,073 independent OASIS-3 subjects confirmed cross-cohort generalisability (H=166.99, p<0.0001, eta^2=0.156). A mechanistic comparison with SHAP demonstrated that ablation-based MCS captures clinically meaningful modality dependence that deviation-based methods cannot. These findings position MCNet as a foundation for personalised imaging decisions, clinical trial stratification, and trustworthy AI in dementia care.

</details>


### [234] [Improving Cross-Site Whole-Heart Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25109)
*Tanish Mudaliar,Justin Li,Daniel Lin,Julianna Vo,Kaitao Liao,Xin Wang,Shu Hu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模态路由的3D心脏分割流程，通过结合站点特征化的外观增强策略（偏置场+贝塞尔曲线），显著提升了全心脏分割模型在多中心、多模态分布偏移下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多中心和多模态分布偏移下全心脏分割的挑战，特别是在训练数据有限且测试站点 unseen 的情况下，图像间距、强度、重建纹理和解剖结构的差异会导致模型性能下降。

Method: 提出一种模态路由的3D分割流程：1) 使用TotalSegmentator初始化的nnU-Netv2模型；2) 基于可测量的图像属性对可用站点进行特征分析，以此指导数据空间泛化策略；3) 应用“偏置场+贝塞尔曲线”外观增强，结合平滑空间强度扰动与非线性强度重映射；4) 辅以轻量级的类别最大连通分量清理后处理。

Result: 在主要保留站点验证集上，CT平均Dice系数从0.8350提升至0.9135，MRI平均Dice系数从0.7695提升至0.7830，同时降低了HD95（95%豪斯多夫距离）。

Conclusion: 基于站点特征 motivated 的外观增强是一种实用策略，能有效提升有限数据条件下全心脏分割的跨站点鲁棒性。

Abstract: Whole-heart segmentation from CT and MRI is essential for quantitative cardiac image analysis, but remains challenging under multi-center and multi-modality distribution shift. In the CARE whole-heart segmentation task, models must generalize from limited labeled sites to unseen acquisition distributions, where variation in spacing, intensity, reconstruction texture, and anatomy can degrade out-of-distribution performance. We propose a modality-routed 3D cardiac segmentation pipeline that combines TotalSegmentator-initialized nnU-Netv2 models with site-characterized, label-preserving appearance augmentation. We first characterize the available sites using measurable image properties and use this analysis to motivate candidate data-space generalization routes. The final retained recipe applies Bias Field + Bezier appearance augmentation, combining smooth spatial intensity perturbation with nonlinear intensity remapping, followed by lightweight class-wise largest-connected-component cleanup. On the primary held-out-site validation splits, the final configuration improves CT mean Dice from 0.8350 to 0.9135 and MRI mean Dice from 0.7695 to 0.7830, while also reducing HD95. These results suggest that site-motivated appearance augmentation is a practical strategy for improving cross-site robustness in limited-data whole-heart segmentation. Our code can be found in https://github.com/Purdue-M2/Improving-Cross-Site-Whole-Heart-Segmentation

</details>


### [235] [Learning spatially varying regularisation parameters of low regularity for image reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25127)
*Kostas Papafitsoros,Luca Calatroni,Andreas Kofler*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文回顾了变分图像重建中空间自适应正则化权重函数的正则性属性，讨论了其在结合模型基正则化（如TV和TGV）与深度神经网络时的作用，并通过图像去噪和MRI重建实例展示了学习权重的低正则性及其对图像结构和噪声的适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期混合图像重建方法结合了基于模型的正则化与深度神经网络以学习高度自适应的正则化权重，需要理解这些权重的结构属性如何从理论和实践角度影响重建效果。

Method: 回顾现有理论文献，分析不同正则性类别（常数、连续、分段常数函数）的权重属性，并通过图像去噪和MRI重建的代表性示例进行验证。

Result: 学习到的权重通常具有低正则性，不仅能适应图像结构，还能适应特定噪声实现，从而显著改善重建中的边缘和细节保留。

Conclusion: 总结了该主题的未来研究方向，强调了理解自适应权重正则性属性对改进混合图像重建方法的重要性。

Abstract: In this chapter, we review and discuss the regularity properties of spatially adaptive regularisation weight functions used in variational image reconstruction. Incorporating such weights into classical model-based regularisers, such as Total Variation (TV) and Total Generalised Variation (TGV), allows the regularisation strength to vary across the image and adapt to local image content. When appropriately estimated, these weights can thus significantly improve edge and detail preservation in the reconstructions. We review the existing theoretical literature on this topic for different regularity classes, including constant, continuous, and piecewise constant functions. Our discussion is motivated by recent work on hybrid image reconstruction methods that combine model-based regularisation with deep neural networks to learn highly adaptive regularisation weights. In particular, we discuss how the structural properties of these weights influence the reconstruction from both theoretical and practical perspectives. Through representative examples in image denoising and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) reconstruction, we demonstrate that the learned weights are often of low regularity and can adapt not only to the image structure but also to the specific noise realisation. We conclude by highlighting several directions for future research on this topic.

</details>


<div id='cs.CC'></div>

# cs.CC [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [Pseudorandom Functions in $\mathsf{NC}^1$ from LWE/LPN/CDH (Or: How to Build PRFs in $\mathsf{NC}^1$, Generically)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25213)
*Youlong Ding,Aayush Jain,Ilan Komargodski*

Main category: cs.CC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的通用转换方法，将深度为 $Ω(\log n)$ 的弱伪随机函数（PRF）转换为深度为 $O(d(n))$ 的强 PRF，并基于多种标准假设构建了新的深度高效弱 PRF，首次实现了基于标准 LWE、LPN 和 CDH 假设的 $\mathsf{NC}^1$ 可计算强 PRF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长期存在的开放性问题，即在标准密码学假设（如标准 LWE、LPN 和 CDH）下构建低深度（特别是 $\mathsf{NC}^1$ 类）的强伪随机函数。 prior 工作要么依赖更强的假设（如 Ring-LWE 超多项式比率、结构化 LPN 变体或 DDH/GDH），要么无法在保持低深度的同时满足强安全性要求。

Method: 提出一种改进的 GGM 树型范式，通过“渐缩”（tapering）内部状态使每一层的深度呈几何级数递减，从而实现从弱 PRF 到强 PRF 的高效通用转换；同时结合基于多种标准假设的新建深度高效弱 PRF 构造。
