Result: 1. 首次基于多项式模噪比的标准 LWE 假设构建 $\mathsf{NC}^1$-PRF，优于 prior 需要 Ring-LWE 且超多项式比率的构造；2. 首次基于标准 LPN 假设构建 $\mathsf{NC}^1$-PRF，无需结构化 LPN 变体；3. 首次基于 CDH 假设（而非更强的 DDH 或 GDH）构建 $\mathsf{NC}^1$-PRF。

Conclusion: 该工作通过创新的“渐缩”状态转换技术和新型弱 PRF 构造，成功在多种经典标准假设下实现了 $\mathsf{NC}^1$ 可计算的强伪随机函数，解决了密码学复杂度理论中的多个长期开放问题，显著降低了强 PRF 构造对假设强度和计算深度的要求。

Abstract: We present a new generic transformation from weak PRFs computable in depth $d(n) = Ω(\log n)$ to strong PRFs computable in depth $O(d(n))$. This construction refines the classical tree-based paradigm of GGM by {tapering} the internal state so the per-level depth decreases geometrically. We complement the above with new depth-efficient weak PRF constructions based on various standard assumptions.
  As a corollary, we obtain new $\mathsf{NC}^1$-computable PRFs from various classical assumptions, resolving several long-standing open problems. Concretely, for the first time, we obtain $\mathsf{NC}^1$-computable PRFs:
  (1) from the \textbf{Learning With Errors (LWE)} assumption with a polynomial modulus-to-noise ratio, improving upon prior low-depth constructions that required Ring-LWE with super-polynomial ratios [Banerjee-Peikert-Rosen, EUROCRYPT 2012];
  (2)from the standard \textbf{Learning Parity with Noise (LPN)} assumption, removing the need for structured LPN variants [Boyle et al., FOCS 2020], [Ding-Jain-Komargodski, STOC 2025];
  (3) from the \textbf{Computational Diffie-Hellman (CDH)} assumption; prior works relied on the stronger Decisional Diffie-Hellman (DDH) or generalized Diffie-Hellman (GDH) assumptions [Naor-Reingold, FOCS '97, J. ACM '04].

</details>


### [237] [Continuous Computational Social Choice: A Case Study in Bribery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25444)
*Martin Koutecký,Nikolaos Melissinos,Tung Anh Vu,Lluís Sabater*

Main category: cs.CC

TL;DR: 本文提出在“社会连续统”（无限微主体分布）设定下研究计算社会选择问题，发现许多在离散设定中NP难的选举攻击问题（如Borda和Bucklin控制、单位成本Borda交换贿赂）在此设定下变为多项式时间可解，但部分带有一般成本函数的问题仍保持计算困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算社会选择主要将社会建模为离散主体集合，而本文受物理学（统计力学）和经济学（平均场博弈）启发，旨在探索将社会建模为连续统（无限微主体分布）时的算法特性，以填补该设定下的研究空白。

Method: 采用连续与离散优化相结合的技术，包括配置线性规划（Configuration LP）框架和动态规划。在硬度证明中，创新性地使用了“反转硬度流”技术，揭示了线性规划公式与定价问题之间的复杂性关系。

Result: 1. 广泛的标准选举攻击类在社会连续统中变为多项式时间可解，包括离散设定中NP难的Borda-CCDV、Bucklin-CCDV及单位成本Borda-SWAP BRIBERY。
2. 对于$k$-Approval-SWAP BRIBERY，当$k$为常数（一般成本）或$k$变化且成本函数可加分离时，给出了多项式时间算法；后者在离散设定中对固定$k\ge 2$被证明为NP完全。
3. 证明带有一般成本函数的Borda-SWAP BRIBERY和$k$-Approval-SWAP BRIBERY在社会连续统中仍保持计算困难。

Conclusion: 社会连续统模型显著改变了选举攻击问题的计算复杂性景观，使许多离散难解问题变得可解，揭示了建模粒度对算法结果的关键影响，同时确立了特定一般成本问题在该设定下的固有难度。

Abstract: Computational social choice seeks algorithmic answers to questions about preference aggregation, safety of elections, robustness of outcomes, stability, etc. It overwhelmingly models societies as composed of discrete agents.
  We propose to study computational social choice problems in a society continuum} setting, where a society is modeled as a distribution of infinitely many infinitesimal agents of different types. An analogous approach has been very useful in physics (it is the basis of statistical mechanics), economics (mean field games), and other fields.
  As an initial case study, we focus on election attacks (bribery and control), which have been extensively studied in the discrete setting. We show that a broad class of standard election attacks becomes polynomial-time solvable in the society continuum. The class contains problems that are NP-hard discretely, among them Borda- and Bucklin-CCDV and unit-cost Borda-SWAP BRIBERY. Furthermore, we give polynomial-time algorithms for $k$-Approval-SWAP BRIBERY when $k$ is constant for general costs, and when $k$ varies and the cost function is additively separable. The latter result contrasts with the discrete problem, which we prove NP-complete for additively separable costs and every fixed $k\ge 2$. In contrast, we prove that Borda-SWAP BRIBERY and $k$-Approval-SWAP BRIBERY, both with general costs, remain computationally hard in the society continuum.
  To obtain these results, we use both continuous and discrete optimization techniques, such as the Configuration LP framework and dynamic programming. Of particular note is the technique underlying our hardness proofs, which shows how to ''reverse the flow of hardness'' between LP formulations and pricing problems.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [238] [Towards Faithful and Efficient Semantic Communication: An Ontological Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25422)
*Yixiao Feng,Yueting Wang,Yining Wang,Han Han,Bo Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种本体驱动的语义通信框架（ODSC），用于多视角视觉问答任务，通过共享本体知识库减少冗余、消除歧义并检测不一致性，从而在降低87.1%数据量的同时提升4.5%的问答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多视角视觉问答任务中，基于视觉语言模型（VLMs）提取的场景图存在冗余、歧义和不一致的问题，主要源于VLMs的完整性、异构性和不可解释性，导致通信效率低下和语义理解困难。

Method: 提出本体驱动的语义通信（ODSC）框架：发送端和接收端共享基于本体的知识库（预定义同义词、推理规则和一致性约束）；发送端利用推理规则去除可推断的部分场景图以减少冗余；接收端基于同义词对齐不同视角的语义信息，并基于约束检测视角间的不一致性。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与传输完整场景图相比，该框架将语义信息数据量减少高达87.1%，同时问答准确率提升4.5%；与语义信息过滤方法相比，多视角视觉问答准确率（MVA）提升高达16.0%。

Conclusion: ODSC框架通过本体知识库有效解决了多视角场景图的冗余、歧义和不一致问题，显著提升了通信效率和问答性能，为多视角视觉问答任务提供了一种高效的语义通信解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, an ontology-driven semantic communication (ODSC) framework is proposed for multi-view visual question answering (VQA) tasks. In the considered framework, multiple transmitters observe a scene, extract the semantic information (SI) with vision-language models (VLMs), and transmit the scene graphs to a receiver. Due to the completeness, heterogeneity, and uninterpretability of the VLMs, the extracted scene graphs are redundant, ambiguous, and inconsistent. To solve these problems, the transmitters and the receiver share an ontology-based knowledge base that predefines synonyms, inference rules, and consistency constraints. For each transmitter, the proposed ODSC framework removes the partial scene graph that can be inferred based on the inference rules. For the receiver, the proposed framework aligns the SI of different views based on the synonyms and detects the inconsistency among the views based on the constraints. A metric of multi-view VQA accuracy (MVA) is defined to evaluate the proposed framework. Simulation results show that, compared with transmitting the complete scene graphs, the proposed framework reduces the data size of the SI by up to 87.1% while improving the answering accuracy by 4.5%. Moreover, the proposed framework yields up to a 16.0% improvement in terms of the MVA compared with the SI filtering approaches.

</details>


### [239] [Conditional Total Correlation and the Serial Depth of Adaptive Parallel Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25505)
*Chuling Wen,Weijie Liang,Jian Lu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了掩码扩散模型中的自适应并行采样，提出了一个精确恒等式：策略的散度等于揭示轮次中积累的条件总相关性的期望，从而将条件总相关性确定为并行化的精确信息成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受掩码扩散模型中并行解码的启发，研究离散向量的自适应并行采样问题，旨在理解并行化过程中的信息成本及其对采样效率的影响。

Method: 通过定义确定性策略选择未揭示坐标并独立采样，使用前向Kullback-Leibler散度衡量近似误差，以串行深度（满足误差预算所需的最小平均轮次）作为评估指标，推导出散度与条件总相关性之间的精确恒等式。

Result: 1. 有限阶马尔可夫链的零误差调度轮次复杂度与马尔可夫阶数成正比，与序列长度成对数关系；2. Bernoulli游走在固定误差预算下具有匹配的对数特征；3. 左右顺序与层次揭示顺序之间存在线性与对数的分离；4. 均匀随机排列需要线性期望轮次；5. 均匀平衡二进制字符串深度为对数平方阶，二进制one-hot块深度为平方根阶。

Conclusion: 条件依赖结构是并行化能力的根本决定因素，串行深度与熵和负对数似然分离，实验表明伪成本能区分解码规则且策略排名与自采样输出质量高度一致。

Abstract: Motivated by parallel decoding in masked diffusion models, we study adaptive parallel sampling of discrete vectors: in each round, a deterministic policy selects unrevealed coordinates on the basis of the values observed so far, and the selected coordinates are sampled independently from their exact conditional marginals. Approximation error is measured by forward Kullback-Leibler divergence, and serial depth is the minimum target-averaged number of rounds meeting a prescribed error budget. Our central result is an exact identity: the divergence of every policy equals the expected conditional total correlation accumulated over its reveal rounds, so conditional total correlation is the exact information cost of within-round parallelism. The identity yields zero-error schedules for finite-order Markov chains with round complexity proportional to the Markov order and logarithmic in sequence length, a matching logarithmic characterization of the Bernoulli walk at every fixed error budget, and a linear-versus-logarithmic separation between left-to-right and hierarchical reveal orders. Uniform random permutations require linearly many expected rounds at every fixed budget; their hard-cap round-error tradeoff is an exact integer-composition problem whose fixed-round asymptotics and joint-scaling frontier we determine. Uniform balanced binary strings have depth of order squared logarithm, and binary one-hot blocks have square-root depth, with rectangular versions realizing every polynomial exponent up to one half. These results separate serial depth from entropy and negative log-likelihood, and establish conditional-dependence structure as a fundamental determinant of parallelizability. Experiments with a masked diffusion language model show that the pseudo-cost distinguishes deployed decoding rules and that its policy rankings agree closely with the quality of self-sampled outputs.

</details>


### [240] [Joint Beamforming Design and Port Selection in Fluid Antenna-Assisted Multi-Cell Networks: A Personalized Federated Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25514)
*Liwen Gao,Li Zheng,Xing Hao,Ziru Chen,Lin X. Cai*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于联邦表征学习（FedRep）和位置感知双分支深度神经网络（PA-DNN）的框架，用于在多小区流体天线辅助网络中联合优化波束成形与端口选择，以最大化加权总和速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多小区流体天线辅助（FAS）网络中，有源波束成形与离散流体天线端口选择通过小区内和小区间干扰紧密耦合，传统方法难以有效联合优化以最大化加权总和速率（WSR），且需要在异构小区间实现去中心化训练。

Method: 开发了一种联邦表征学习（FedRep）框架，内含位置感知双分支深度神经网络（PA-DNN）。该网络以信道状态信息和端口位置编码为输入，通过两个特定任务分支联合输出波束成形向量和端口选择方案。在训练策略上，基站间共享全局波束成形相关参数，同时保留端口选择参数以实现小区特定的本地自适应。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与传统的联邦学习（FL）方案及端口选择基准方案相比，所提出的方案实现了更高的加权总和速率。

Conclusion: 该研究证实了利用FedRep框架结合PA-DNN进行联合波束成形和端口选择的有效性，能够显著提升多小区FAS网络的频谱效率，并成功解决了异构环境下的去中心化优化问题。

Abstract: This paper investigates joint beamforming and port selection in multi-cell fluid antenna-assisted (FAS) networks. In such networks, active beamforming and discrete FA port selection are coupled through intra-cell and inter-cell interference and are jointly optimized to maximize the weighted sum-rate (WSR). We develop a federated representation learning (FedRep) framework with a position-aware dual-branch deep neural network (PA-DNN). The PA-DNN uses channel state information and port positional encoding as inputs, and jointly outputs beamforming vectors and port selections through two task-specific branches. To support decentralized training across heterogeneous cells, the FedRep framework shares global beamforming-related parameters among base stations while keeping port-selection parameters local for cell-specific adaptation. Simulation results show that the proposed scheme achieves a higher weighted sum-rate than conventional FL and port-selection benchmark schemes.

</details>


### [241] [Constrained Maximum Entropy Contiguous Aggregations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25533)
*Roberto Bruno,Ugo Vaccaro*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对给定概率分布和熵值上限，提出了计算最大熵连续聚合的精确动态规划算法及两种高效贪心近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在约束条件下（香农熵不超过给定值R），如何将原始概率分布聚合为项数更少的连续分布，同时最大化其熵值的问题，该问题在特定应用场景中具有实际意义。

Method: 设计了一种精确求解该问题的动态规划算法，并提出了两种时间效率更高的贪心算法以获取接近最优的解。

Result: 实现了在熵值约束下最大熵连续聚合的精确计算，并提供了高效的近似解决方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的算法能有效解决最大熵连续聚合问题，且文中讨论了该问题产生的具体应用场景。

Abstract: Given a probability distribution $p = (p_1, \dots, p_n)$ and an integer $1\leq m \leq n$, a contiguous aggregation of $p$ is a probability distribution $q = (q_1, \dots, q_m)$ such that each $q_i$ is a sum of consecutive elements of $p$. Given $p$ and a positive number $R$, we consider the problem of computing a maximum entropy contiguous aggregation $q$ of $p$, under the constraint that its Shannon entropy $H(q)$ is at most $R$. We devise a dynamic programming algorithm that solves the problem exactly, and two time-efficient greedy algorithms that provide close-to-optimal solutions. We discuss a few scenarios where our problem arises.

</details>


### [242] [Quantitative tiling stability from quadratic discrepancy in Hamming spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25716)
*Valery,Grishin,Aryeh Lev Zabokritskiy*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限汉明空间中二次球差异（quadratic ball discrepancy）与完美码（perfect codes）的关系，证明了平铺缺陷稳定性（tiling-defect stability），即超过完美码基准的差异控制了球覆盖重数与 1 的偏差平方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过能量最小化框架理解完美码的稳定性特性，并建立差异度量与码结构缺陷之间的定量关系，以评估非完美码与完美码基准的偏离程度。

Method: 固定字母表大小、长度和码基数，比较所有具有这些参数的码；利用球 packing 条件和 Lloyd 积分性条件，推导参数依赖的系数界限；针对单错误和双错误参数对分别建立显式正系数估计；结合重复码和 Golay 码族的直接证明及完美码分类，推广至所有非平凡完美码。

Result: 证明了对于满足特定条件的单错误参数，下界系数为 $κ_{n,q}/q^2\geq1$；对于 $n\geq5$ 的双错误参数对，获得了不假设完美码存在的显式正系数；对于大小至少为 4 的字母表，该条件系数具有闭式表达和固定字母表渐近性；差异缺陷还对应于均匀球噪声下的归一化卡方平滑误差。

Conclusion: 二次球差异的超额部分控制了球覆盖重数分布的稳定性，进而控制孔洞、重叠、缺陷环境点、总变差及 Rényi 散度等统计量；该稳定性不要求竞争码具有线性结构或满足特定距离约束，为码理论中的能量最小化提供了新的分析视角。

Abstract: Quadratic ball discrepancy defines an energy on codes in finite Hamming spaces. At perfect-code parameters, its exact minimizers are the perfect codes. We fix the alphabet size, length, and code cardinality and compare all codes with these parameters. We prove tiling-defect stability: excess discrepancy above the perfect-code benchmark controls the squared deviation of the distinguished ball-covering multiplicity from one. For one-error parameters satisfying sphere-packing and Lloyd integrality, the lower coefficient is $κ_{n,q}/q^2\geq1$. The uniform floor one is sharp, while the certified parameter-dependent coefficient can be much larger. An explicit parameter-dependent upper estimate is also available, and the two certified coefficients can be far apart. For any two-error parameter pair with $n\geq5$ satisfying sphere-packing divisibility and having two distinct integral Lloyd roots in the Hamming weight range, we obtain an explicit positive coefficient without assuming that a perfect code exists. For alphabets of size at least four, this conditional coefficient has a closed form and fixed-alphabet asymptotics. Direct certificates for the repetition and Golay families, combined with perfect-code classification, give tiling-defect stability for every nontrivial perfect code. Here stability concerns the ball-covering multiplicity profile, not symmetric-difference proximity to a particular perfect code. The defect is also a normalized chi-square smoothing error under uniform ball noise, so excess discrepancy controls holes, overlaps, defective ambient points, total variation, and Rényi divergence from uniformity of the ball-noise output. Competing codes need not be linear or satisfy a distance or error-correction constraint.

</details>


### [243] [Beyond Minimum Distance: The Optimal Leading Coefficient in the High-SNR Error-Probability Expansion for AWGN Spherical Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25805)
*Nikola Zlatanov*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在高信噪比（SNR）下，针对每个SNR值重新优化球码（spherical codes）比固定码本能获得更小的误码率主导系数，从而在保持最优错误指数的同时进一步降低精确误码概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 packing-optimal $(M,n)$ 球码虽然在高 SNR 下能达到最优错误指数，但其误码概率的主导系数 $K_{M,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}$ 并非最小。研究旨在探究是否可以通过随 SNR 动态调整码本，获得更小的主导系数 $K_{M,n}^\ast$，从而在所有足够高的 SNR 下实现更低的精确最大似然误码概率。

Method: 通过理论推导比较固定码本的最小误码概率 $P_e^{\mathrm{fix}}$ 与逐 SNR 优化码本的最小误码概率 $P_e^\ast$ 的渐近展开式。利用正交多面体界（orthoplex-bound）构造法，引入依赖于 SNR 的微扰策略：使一类极限最近邻对略微靠近，同时将剩余最近邻对在更大但仍趋于零的尺度上移远，从而消除后者对主导系数的贡献。此外，将 $K_{M,n}^\ast$ 表征为最小高斯软包装能量（Gaussian soft-packing energy）的极限。

Result: 1. 证明了 $K_{M,n}^\ast \leq K_{M,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}$，且严格不等式成立时，$P_e^\ast < P_e^{\mathrm{fix}}$。
2. 对于 $2\leq M\leq n+1$，两者相等，均为 $M(M-1)$。
3. 对于 $M=n+k$ ($2\leq k\leq n$)，证明了 $K_{n+k,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}=4n(k-1)$ 且 $K_{n+k,n}^\ast\leq 4k(k-1)$。
4. 特例显示，当 $M=n+2$ 且 $n\geq3$ 时，$K_{n+2,n}^\ast=8$ 而 $K_{n+2,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}=4n$，SNR 逐点优化将渐近误差改善了 $n/2$ 倍。
5. 提出了关于 $3\leq k\leq n-1$ 时 $K_{n+k,n}^\ast=4k(k-1)$ 的猜想。

Conclusion: 在高 SNR 区域，逐 SNR 优化码本策略优于固定 packing-optimal 码本策略。通过特定的微扰构造，可以显著降低误码概率的主导系数，特别是在码字数量 $M$ 略大于维度 $n$ 的情况下，这种优化能带来数量级上的性能提升，揭示了动态码本设计在精确误码率分析中的关键作用。

Abstract: Packing-optimal $(M,n)$ spherical codes attain the largest achievable minimum distance and hence the optimal error exponent at high SNR. Among these codebooks held fixed as SNR grows, the smallest leading coefficient, $K_{M,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}$, equals the smallest number of ordered closest pairs. We show that reoptimizing the codebook at every SNR can yield a smaller leading coefficient $K_{M,n}^\ast$. Specifically, we show that the SNR-wise minimum exact maximum-likelihood error probability is $P_e^\ast=B_γ^\ast(K_{M,n}^\ast+o(1))$, while the minimum exact error over packing-optimal codebooks is $P_e^{\mathrm{fix}}=B_γ^\ast(K_{M,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}+o(1))$, where $B_γ^\ast$ is a common factor. We prove that $K_{M,n}^\ast\leq K_{M,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}$. Strict inequality, $K_{M,n}^\ast < K_{M,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}$ therefore gives a smaller exact error at all sufficiently high SNRs, i.e., $P_e^\ast<P_e^{\mathrm{fix}}$. We also characterize $K_{M,n}^\ast$ as the limit of the minimum Gaussian soft-packing energy.
  An orthoplex-bound construction explains strict inequality: an SNR-dependent perturbation makes one class of limiting closest pairs slightly closer, without changing the optimal exponent or their unit contributions to the constructed family's leading coefficient, while moving the remaining closest pairs farther apart on a larger but still vanishing scale, causing their contributions to vanish.
  For $2\leq M\leq n+1$, we prove $K_{M,n}^\ast=K_{M,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}=M(M-1)$. For $M=n+k$, $2\leq k\leq n$, we prove $K_{n+k,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}=4n(k-1)$ and $K_{n+k,n}^\ast\leq 4k(k-1)$. In particular, $K_{n+2,n}^\ast=8<4n=K_{n+2,n}^{\mathrm{fix}}$ for $n\geq3$, so SNR-wise optimization improves the asymptotic error by the factor $n/2$. At $k=n$, both coefficients equal $4n(n-1)$. For $3\leq k\leq n-1$, we conjecture $K_{n+k,n}^\ast=4k(k-1)$.

</details>


### [244] [Exact Common Information and Exact Channel Synthesis for Correlated Gaussian Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26012)
*Lei Yu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文解决了Yu和Tan在2020年提出的两个关于高斯信源精确公共信息和精确信道合成的猜想，证明了精确公共信息严格大于Wyner公共信息，且精确信道合成所需速率严格高于全变分版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Yu和Tan在IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory上提出的两个未决猜想，明确$\rho$-相关高斯信源的精确公共信息表达式及精确信道合成中共享随机性与通信速率的允许区域，以厘清精确版本与近似版本（如Wyner公共信息和全变分版本）在速率需求上的本质差异。

Method: 结合最坏情况高斯交叉熵的精确最优传输表示、Fathi高斯传输不等式以及由条件均值协方差结构导出的行列式不等式进行证明。

Result: 1) 确立了$\rho$-相关高斯信源对的精确公共信息为$\frac{1}{2}\log\frac{1+\rho}{1-\rho}+\frac{\rho}{1+\rho}$；2) 确定了精确信道合成中共享随机性速率与通信速率的允许区域即为猜想区域。

Conclusion: 对于任意$\rho>0$，相关高斯信源对的精确公共信息严格大于Wyner公共信息；且其精确信道合成所需的速率严格高于全变分版本，揭示了精确合成在信息论资源消耗上的更高要求。

Abstract: In this paper, we resolve two conjectures posed by Yu and Tan in 2020 (in two separate papers published in the IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory). Specifically, we establish that: 1) the exact common information for a pair of $ρ$-correlated Gaussian sources is given by the conjectured expression $\frac{1}{2}\log\frac{1+ρ}{1-ρ}+\fracρ{1+ρ}$; and 2) the admissible region for the shared randomness rate and the communication rate in exact channel synthesis is exactly the conjectured one. These results yield two important consequences. First, for any $ρ>0$, the exact common information of a correlated Gaussian pair strictly exceeds Wyner's common information. Second, for $ρ>0$, the exact channel synthesis of such a pair requires strictly higher rates than the total-variation version. The proof combines an exact optimal-transport representation of the worst-case Gaussian cross-entropy, Fathi's Gaussian transport inequality, and a determinant inequality arising from the covariance structure of the conditional means.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [245] [Dynamic Influence-Weighted Distillation for Single-IMU Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24904)
*Bingxuan Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态影响加权（DIW）方法，利用训练阶段的多传感器（4个IMU）教师模型知识，显著提升仅使用单传感器（右臂IMU）的学生模型在人体活动识别中的性能，且无需改变部署时的硬件配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然多位置惯性传感器能提升活动识别准确率，但在推理阶段要求佩戴所有传感器会增加部署负担。研究旨在解决如何利用训练时可用的多传感器数据，来优化仅在推理时使用单传感器（右臂IMU）的轻量级模型，以平衡性能与部署便捷性。

Method: 引入动态影响加权（DIW）策略改进固定权重知识蒸馏。DIW通过在折叠内训练参与者上测试单步候选更新，为每个样本的Logit损失和特征损失分配独立的动态权重门控，从而替代对所有样本施加相同强度的固定权重蒸馏，实现更高效的知识迁移。

Result: 在WEAR数据集（22名参与者，19类标签）上的主体不相交五折交叉验证显示：DIW方法的宏观F1分数达到0.638451，相比监督学习（0.561820）和固定权重知识蒸馏（0.571623）分别提升了7.66和6.68个百分点。DIW在19个标签中的18个以及22名持出参与者中的21名上均优于监督学习方法。

Conclusion: DIW成功将仅在训练阶段可用的多位置传感器信息转化为更强的单IMU模型性能，在不改变部署 sensing 配置或学生模型前向图的前提下，显著解决了单传感器模型性能受限的问题，实现了高精度与低部署负担的统一。

Abstract: Inertial sensors at multiple body locations can improve activity recognition, but requiring every sensor at inference increases the deployment burden. We study whether four synchronized IMUs available during training can improve a student that uses only the right-arm IMU during fitting and inference. A frozen four-IMU teacher provides logit and feature targets. Fixed-weight knowledge distillation applies each target with the same strength to every fitting sample, although the student may not benefit equally from them. We introduce dynamic influence weighting (DIW), which tests a one-step candidate update on separate fold-internal training participants. DIW then assigns separate sample-wise gates to the logit and feature losses. On WEAR, we evaluate 19 labels and 68,298 complete windows from 22 participants using subject-disjoint five-fold cross-validation. Pooled out-of-fold macro-F1 is 0.561820 for Supervised and 0.571623 for Fixed-weight KD. DIW reaches 0.638451, gains of 7.66 and 6.68 percentage points, respectively. It exceeds Supervised for 18 of 19 labels and 21 of 22 held-out participants. All three routes retain the same 80,915-parameter right-arm student at inference. Under this protocol, DIW converts training-only multi-position information into a stronger single-IMU model without changing deployed sensing or the student forward graph.

</details>


### [246] [GreenLeaf Law Embed Tiny: A Compact Embedding Model for Legal Domain Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24936)
*Surya Saka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了GreenLeaf Law Embed Tiny，一个0.6B参数的法律领域嵌入模型，通过两阶段训练和高质量数据集，在资源受限环境下实现了具有竞争力的法律检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在资源受限环境中部署高效法律领域检索模型的需求，证明通过特定领域训练和高质量数据可以提升专用领域应用的性能。

Method: 采用两阶段训练流程：首先从大型教师模型向紧凑学生架构蒸馏知识，随后利用硬负例挖掘进行领域特定微调；使用包含340万查询-段落对（含15万人工 curated 样本）的精心策划数据集；构建支持多种量化级别（BF16, INT8, binary）的高效推理架构。

Result: 在大规模法律嵌入基准（MLEB）上达到75.11%，在MTEB(Law, v1)上达到64.38%，在1B参数以下模型中表现具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 领域特定训练结合高质量数据能显著提升专用领域（如法律）应用的性能，且该模型架构支持在资源受限环境中的高效部署。

Abstract: We present GreenLeaf Law Embed Tiny, a 0.6B parameter embedding model for legal domain retrieval. GreenLeaf-Tiny achieves 75.11% on the Massive Legal Embedding Benchmark (MLEB) and 64.38% on MTEB(Law, v1),demonstrating competitive performance among models under 1B parameters. Our approach combines a two-stage training pipeline that first distills knowledge from a larger teacher model into a compact student architecture, then applies domain-specific fine-tuning with hard negative mining; a carefully curated dataset of 3.4 million query-passage pairs, including 150,000 human-curated samples across diverse legal jurisdictions; and an efficient inference architecture supporting multiple quantization levels (BF16, INT8, binary) enabling deployment in resource-constrained environments. We provide detailed analysis of our training methodology, architectural choices, and comprehensive evaluation across legal retrieval tasks. Our results demonstrate that domain-specific training with high-quality data can improve performance for specialized domain applications

</details>


### [247] [Multi-Modal Anomaly Detection: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24937)
*Xudong Mou,Zexin Wu,Chuan Luo,Shiru Chen,Xudong Liu,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从假设驱动的角度综述多模态异常检测（MMAD），将其方法划分为基于正常性假设和基于异常性假设两大范式，并探讨了基础模型对该领域的影响及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MMAD文献在不同领域和模态组合中呈现碎片化，且既有综述多按架构分类，缺乏从“异常如何被定义和分离”这一核心角度进行的系统性梳理。

Method: 形式化定义MMAD问题，识别其核心挑战的五个内在特征，并将现有工作组织为两个互补范式：1）正常性假设方法（通过表征学习、跨模态对齐和知识增强建模规律性）；2）异常性假设方法（通过粗粒度、结构和语义的异常注入来锐化决策边界）。同时分析基础模型如何通过预训练、跨模态迁移和推理能力重塑该领域。

Result: 构建了基于假设的分类框架，整理了跨领域的代表性基准与评估协议，并阐明了基础模型在可扩展预训练、灵活跨模态转移及新兴推理能力方面对MMAD的推动作用。

Conclusion: 文章指出了构建鲁棒、自适应且可解释的MMAD系统所面临的开放性问题，并提出了未来的研究方向。

Abstract: Multi-Modal Anomaly Detection (MMAD) detects rare abnormal events from heterogeneous data sources and is increasingly used in safety- and reliability-critical applications such as industrial inspection and cybersecurity. Yet the literature is fragmented across domains and modality combinations, and existing surveys usually group methods by architecture rather than by how abnormality is defined and separated in multi-modal settings. We survey MMAD from an assumption-driven perspective. We formalize the problem, identify five intrinsic characteristics underlying its core challenges, and organize prior work into two complementary paradigms. The first, normality-assumption methods, models regularity via representation learning, cross-modal alignment, and knowledge enhancement. The second, anomaly-assumption methods, sharpens decision boundaries through coarse-grained, structural, and semantic anomaly injection. We also investigate how foundation models are reshaping MMAD through scalable pretraining, flexible cross-modal transfer, and emerging reasoning capabilities. Finally, we compile representative benchmarks and evaluation protocols across domains and highlight open problems and future directions for robust, adaptive, and interpretable MMAD systems.

</details>


### [248] [ExFold: Unified Expert Folding for Training-Free MoE Prefill-Decode Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24938)
*Juntong Wu,Yifei Liu,Junyi Chen,Siqi Fan,Chaoran Feng,Minghao Li,Liujie Zhang,Weihang Chen,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ExFold框架，通过专家折叠机制统一加速MoE模型的prefill和decode阶段，在保持99%质量的同时实现1.41倍TTFT和2.45倍TPOT加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型低延迟服务面临挑战，因为prefill和decode阶段存在不同的瓶颈：prefill受token级专家计算主导，decode受batch级激活专家的内存流量限制。现有无训练加速方法仅优化单一资源代理，且丢弃或隐式近似被排除专家的贡献。

Method: 将prefill和decode统一为预算化输出近似问题，仅执行特定阶段约束的专家集，同时使用校准的标量投影器将被排除专家的贡献投影到保留专家上。基于专家输出方向对齐但幅度不同的观察，在无标签数据上校准成对标量投影器矩阵。

Result: 在vLLM中实现即插即用插件，包含轻量级专家折叠CUDA内核，实现最高1.41倍TTFT和2.45倍TPOT加速，同时保留约99%的原始平均质量。

Conclusion: ExFold通过共享折叠机制统一处理prefill和decode阶段的加速，仅在保留专家选择方式上有所不同，有效解决了MoE服务中的双阶段瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale capacity for strong quality while keeping per-token compute bounded through sparse expert activation. Yet low-latency MoE serving is increasingly challenging, because it spans two inference phases with fundamentally different bottlenecks: prefill is dominated by token-wise expert computation, whereas decode is constrained by memory traffic from the batch-wise activated expert set. However, existing training-free acceleration methods optimize only a single resource proxy, either the experts each token executes or the experts a batch activates, and either discard the excluded experts' contribution or leave it only implicitly approximated. In this paper, we propose ExFold, a unified training-free expert-folding framework for jointly accelerating MoE prefill and decode. ExFold casts both prefill and decode as one budgeted output-approximation problem: execute only a phase-specific constrained expert set while projecting the contribution of budget-excluded experts onto retained experts using calibrated scalar projectors. Motivated by the observation that many expert outputs are directionally aligned but differ in magnitude, ExFold calibrates a pairwise scalar-projector matrix on unlabeled data and uses it at inference time to fold excluded expert contributions into retained experts. Under this view, prefill acceleration becomes token-level Top-K folding, and decode acceleration becomes batch-level expert-pool folding. The two phases differ only in how retained experts are selected, while excluded contributions are recovered by one shared folding mechanism. We implement ExFold as a plug-and-play plugin in vLLM, with a lightweight expert-folding CUDA kernel, delivering up to 1.41x TTFT and 2.45x TPOT speedups while retaining about 99% of the original average quality.

</details>


### [249] [When Does Frequency Decomposition Benefit Physics-Informed Neural Networks? A Preliminary Ablation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24940)
*Shubham Rai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双分支谱门控架构（DBSG-PINN），通过分离低频和高频分量来解决物理信息神经网络（PINN）的谱偏问题，在复杂多尺度PDE上误差降低59.2%，但在平滑问题上收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN存在谱偏问题，难以有效近似高频和多尺度特征，现有傅里叶特征嵌入和正弦激活等技术缺乏对不同频谱区域实际受益情况的系统性验证。

Method: 引入双分支谱门控架构（DBSG-PINN），将低频和高频分量分离到独立子网络并通过自适应门连接，在五个一维基准PDE上进行部分控制的频率分解和谱路由消融实验。

Result: 频率分解在频谱复杂基准上效果显著（多模态波问题相对L2误差降低59.2%），但在平滑PDE上收益甚微；在1D Wave基准上表现不如简单固定组合变体；门控优势随目标解频谱丰富度增加而提升。

Conclusion: 该架构能有效利用频率结构，但效果依赖于问题的频谱特性，目前为探索性研究，需更多训练种子和基准验证模式是否稳定。

Abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) often have high-frequency and multi-scale features that neural networks struggle to approximate. Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) build the governing equations directly into training, but suffer from spectral bias: they learn low-frequency components faster than high-frequency ones. Techniques such as Fourier feature embeddings and sinusoidal activations address this, but most studies assume they help across the board without checking which spectral regimes actually benefit. We introduce a dual-branch, spectrally-gated architecture (DBSG-PINN) that splits low- and high-frequency components into separate subnetworks joined by an adaptive gate, and use it to run a partially controlled ablation of frequency decomposition and spectral routing. We test this on five one-dimensional benchmark PDEs, ranging from smooth, single-scale problems to oscillatory, multi-scale ones. Frequency decomposition helps most on the spectrally complex benchmarks, cutting relative $L_2$ error by up to $59.2\%$ on a multimodal wave problem, but gives little benefit on smoother PDEs. On one benchmark (1D Wave), it performs substantially worse than a simpler fixed-combination variant. The gate's benefit scales with how spectrally rich the target solution is: the full model's advantage over the ablations is largest on multi-scale benchmarks and smallest (or negative) on single-scale ones, consistent with the gate exploiting frequency structure rather than acting as noise,though we do not directly visualize or quantify its spatial activations in this study. All results come from a single training seed across five 1D benchmarks, so we present this as an exploratory study meant to raise questions rather than answer them, and outline the additional seeds and benchmarks needed to test whether the pattern holds.

</details>


### [250] [FAMPWQ: Fisher Information-based Adaptive Mixed Precision Weight Quantization for Effective LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24945)
*Gongwei Lee,Ji Liu,Juncheng Jia,Ji Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FAMPWQ，一种基于Fisher信息的自适应混合精度权重量化方法，通过强化学习动态分配位宽，显著提升LLM在资源受限设备上的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型(LLMs)虽在多个领域取得显著成就，但其过高的资源需求阻碍了在资源受限设备上的部署。传统量化方法因统一位宽或简单启发式敏感性评估导致性能严重下降。

Method: 1. 提出基于Fisher信息的系统模型，用于衡量层对量化的敏感性；2. 设计基于强化学习的位宽分配器，根据Fisher信息敏感性指标生成自适应位宽分配策略。

Result: 在7个模型和5个基准测试上的实验表明，FAMPWQ在PPL（最多降低3.39）、准确率（最多提高6.87%）和LLM-as-a-judge对比（最高76%胜率）方面显著优于7种基线方法。

Conclusion: FAMPWQ通过层自适应权重量化有效解决了LLM在商用GPU上的推理部署问题，为资源受限场景下的大模型应用提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed remarkable achievements of Large Language Models (LLMs) in multiple domains, while the excessive resource requirements of LLMs hinder the deployment on resource-constrained devices. Although model quantization stands out as an effective approach, conventional quantization approaches typically incur severe performance degradation due to uniform bit-width or simple heuristic sensitivity evaluation. In this paper, we propose a novel Fisher information-based Adaptive Mixed Precision Weight Quantization approach, i.e., FAMPWQ, which performs layer-adaptive weight quantization for effective LLM inference on commodity GPUs. First, we propose a system model with a novel Fisher information metric to measure the layer-wise sensitivity to quantization. Second, we propose a reinforcement learning-based bit-width allocator in FAMPWQ, which generates an adaptive bit-width allocation strategy based on the Fisher information sensitivity metric. Extensive experiments on 7 models and 5 benchmarks demonstrate that FAMPWQ significantly outperforms 7 baseline approaches in terms of PPL (up to 3.39 smaller), accuracy (up to 6.87% higher), and LLM-as-a-judge comparison (up to 76% win rate).

</details>


### [251] [MacroAgent: Regularity-Aware Macro Legalization with LLM-Agent-Designed Contour Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24946)
*Jiaxi Jiang,Xufeng Yao,Yuxuan Zhao,Yuntao Lu,Peiyu Liao,Zuodong Zhang,Yibo Lin,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MacroAgent框架，通过四阶段方法（聚类、轮廓生成、模板匹配、簇间优化）解决VLSI设计中宏模块合法化问题，利用大语言模型发现启发式算法，在布局规则性、线长和PPA指标上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代VLSI设计中宏模块占据核心区域，其位置对最终结果质量（QoR）影响重大，宏合法化是确定宏位置的关键步骤。然而现有方法存在鲁棒性不足、计算成本高或忽视宏模块间规则性等问题。

Method: 提出MacroAgent四阶段框架：1）聚类；2）轮廓生成；3）模板匹配；4）簇间优化。创新性地利用大语言模型（LLMs）发现多种有效的启发式规则感知轮廓算法。

Result: 在TILOS和Chipyard基准测试上，相比最先进方法：布局规则性提升2-8倍，全局布线后布线线长减少3%-5%，拥塞度相当，鲁棒性显著提升且运行时间可接受。端到端Cadence Innovus评估显示：相比DREAMPlace基线，布线线长降低2.9%，TNS改善68.3%；集成到Innovus宏放置流程中布线线长降低1.8%。

Conclusion: MacroAgent框架成功生成鲁棒有效的宏合法化算法解决方案，规则性改进转化为实际的PPA增益，在布线线长和时序指标上均取得显著优势。

Abstract: Macros constitute a large part of the core area in modern very large-scale integration (VLSI) designs. Moreover, macro positions have a significant impact on the final quality of result (QoR), and macro legalization is typically the final step in determining the macro positions. However, existing approaches related to macro legalization either lack robustness or incur substantial computational costs or neglect the regularity between macros. To address these limitations, we introduce MacroAgent. The novel framework is a four-stage approach: clustering, contour generation, template matching, and inter-cluster refinement. We propose leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to discover multiple, effective heuristic regularity-aware contour algorithms. This framework successfully generates robust and effective algorithmic solutions for macro legalization. Compared with state-of-the-art macro legalization works, experimental results on TILOS and Chipyard benchmarks demonstrate a 2 to 8 fold improvement in layout regularity, a 3% to 5% reduction in routed wirelength with comparable congestion after global routing, and significantly better robustness with an acceptable runtime. Furthermore, end-to-end evaluation through Cadence Innovus place-and-route confirms that the regularity improvements translate into tangible PPA gains, including 2.9% lower routed wirelength and 68.3% TNS improvement over the DREAMPlace macro legalization baseline; it also achieves 1.8% lower routed wirelength when integrated into the Innovus macro placement flow.

</details>


### [252] [NVExplain: Explaining Time Series Forecasting with Latent Trajectory Analysis and Structure-Preserving Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25080)
*Muyan Anna Li,Manikandan Ravikiran,Aditi Gautam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模型无关的时间序列预测可解释性框架，通过将预测归因于历史滞后，并引入语义流来量化信息在时间上的演变，从而提供针对特定预测范围的、时间上一致的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列预测后验解释方法往往忽略时间依赖性，且无法提供针对特定预测范围的解释，导致高利害场景下的预测难以解释。

Method: 提出模型无关的可解释性框架，将预测建模为潜在轨迹，引入语义流量化信息演变，构建滞后 - 范围归因矩阵，并通过生成结构保持扰动和拟合稀疏局部代理模型来生成人类可读的解释。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上评估，语义流变体在忠实度上达到或优于标准后验基线，且计算效率更高；稳定性分析表明解释具有鲁棒性，并识别出需谨慎应用解释的区域。

Conclusion: 该框架通过语义流和结构保持扰动，有效解决了时间序列预测中时间依赖性和范围特定解释的问题，提供了高效、鲁棒且可理解的解释方法。

Abstract: Time series forecasting models are widely used in high-stakes settings, yet their predictions remain difficult to interpret because existing post-hoc methods often ignore temporal dependence and fail to provide horizon-specific explanations. We propose a model-agnostic explainability framework that explains forecasting predictions by attributing each forecast horizon to temporally relevant historical lags. The framework models forecasting as a latent trajectory and introduces semantic flow to quantify how information evolves across time in the model's internal representations. By aggregating semantic flow, it constructs a lag-horizon attribution matrix that captures horizon-resolved temporal influence. To improve explainability, we further generate structure-preserving perturbations and fit sparse local surrogate models, producing human-readable and temporally coherent explanations. We evaluate the method using faithfulness and stability diagnostics across multiple benchmark datasets. Results show that the semantic-flow variant achieves competitive or superior faithfulness compared to standard post-hoc baselines, while being substantially more computationally efficient. Stability analysis further demonstrates that the explanations are robust and identifies regimes where interpretation should be applied with caution.

</details>


### [253] [CAT-GS: Balanced Multimodal Learning via Calibrated Gating and Fusion Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24947)
*Mahir Shahriar Tamim,Sharjil Khan,Md. Samiul Alim,Tanvir Ahmed Khan,Shafin Rahman,Nabeel Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAT-GS 是一种神经动力学优化控制器，用于解决多模态神经网络训练中的模态不平衡、不稳定门控和融合干扰问题，无需修改模型架构即可提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端多模态神经网络训练常因模态不平衡、门控不稳定和融合干扰三种耦合失效模式导致学习退化，需要一种能稳定神经动力学的优化方法。

Method: CAT-GS 通过温度缩放和 EMA 平滑校准教师可靠性，采用边际阈值策略切换预热丢弃、弱模态优先和弱偏融合，使用梯度预算重归一化稳定梯度幅值，并在融合层应用 PCGrad 减少跨模态干扰。

Result: 在多个音频 - 视觉多模态基准（CREMA-D、AV-MNIST、VGGSound）、三模态（UR-FUNNY）、合成数据及跨域基准上，CAT-GS 相比 OGM-GE、G²D、UMT 等基线提升或匹配融合多模态准确率，门控行为更平滑，融合梯度冲突更少。

Conclusion: CAT-GS 有效解决了多模态训练中的神经动力学不稳定问题，无需修改架构即可在多种场景下实现稳定且高性能的多模态学习。

Abstract: End-to-end training of multimodal neural networks often exhibits unstable neural dynamics characterized by three coupled failure modes that degrade learning: (i) modality imbalance, where one branch dominates gradient-based optimization; (ii) unstable gating, where noisy confidence cues induce erratic modality selection; and (iii) fusion interference, where modality-specific gradients conflict at the shared fusion layer. We propose CAT-GS (Calibrated, Adaptive, Thresholded Gating with Fusion Surgery), a neural dynamics-based optimization controller for intelligent computing applications. CAT-GS operates during backpropagation without modifying model architectures, fusion modules, or task losses. Through calibration of teacher-derived reliability via temperature scaling and EMA smoothing, CAT-GS stabilizes neural dynamics using a margin-thresholded policy to switch between warm-up dropout, weak-modality prioritization, and weak-biased blending, stabilizes gradient magnitudes under aggressive gating via capped gradient-budget renormalization, and applies fusion-only PCGrad to reduce destructive cross-modal interference at the primary shared bottleneck. We evaluate CAT-GS on audio--visual multimodal pattern recognition benchmarks (CREMA-D, AV-MNIST, and VGGSound), a tri-modal setting (UR-FUNNY), controlled synthetic data (CG-MNIST), and additional cross-domain benchmarks (AVE and CMU-MOSI). CAT-GS improves or matches fused multimodal accuracy against strong imbalance-aware baselines (including OGM-GE, G$^2$D, and UMT) across settings, and yields smoother gating behavior with fewer conflicting fusion gradients.

</details>


### [254] [Transforms for LLM Quantization: The Great Inversion and Format Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25188)
*Ehsan Jokar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了当前4-bit LLM量化中“变换阶段”的核心矛盾：经典变换编码追求能量集中，而部署时的分组共享尺度量化追求组内平坦化，两者目标相反（“大反转”原理）。文章系统调研了200篇文献，分类了43种变换方法，并指出最优策略取决于量化格式（如FP4, MXFP4, NVFP4）和分配机制，填补了该领域理论综述的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前竞争性4-bit LLM研究管线普遍采用保持函数的线性变换（如旋转、缩放）来优化异常值分布后再进行舍入，但缺乏针对这一“变换阶段”的专门综述。现有文献在无意中重复推导旧理论，且未形式化其背后的组织原则，导致对变换目标（能量集中 vs. 组内平坦化）及其与不同数字格式交互关系的理解存在缺失。

Method: 1. 理论形式化：提出并证明“大反转”（The Great Inversion）原理，对比经典变换编码（基于Karhunen-Loeve变换的能量集中）与部署矩阵指令（基于Hadamard非相干性的组内平坦化）在主要化（majorization）下的对立目标。
2. 多维分析：引入数字格式（非均匀FP4、MXFP4、NVFP4）作为第二分析轴，评估不同格式下变换目标的偏移。
3. 系统调研：截至2026年6月，调研200篇相关工作，将43种变换方法按结构、数据感知度、搜索/构建方式及运行时成本进行分类。
4. 组合评估：记录变换方法与GPTQ舍入的组合效果，并提炼出针对不同部署机制的首选指南。

Result: 1. 证明了在组内主要化下，能量集中与组内平坦化的优化方向是相反的，且对于通用频谱，一种目标下的最优性无法保证转移到另一种目标。
2. 发现量化格式显著影响变换策略：非均匀FP4网格削弱了平坦化的收益；MXFP4的2的幂次块尺度仍奖励块内旋转；而NVFP4的尾数携带尺度则基本消除了这种拉力。
3. 完成了对43种变换方法的详细分类，并明确了它们在不同部署场景下的适用性。

Conclusion: 4-bit量化的最优变换策略并非单一，而是由“分配机制”与“数字格式”共同决定。研究填补了变换阶段的理论空白，揭示了经典理论与部署现实之间的根本性冲突，并为未来的量化管道设计提供了基于部署机制的分类指南，同时指出了该领域尚待解决的开放性问题。

Abstract: Most competitive 4-bit LLM research pipelines now open the same way: apply a linear, function-preserving transform (rotation, scaling, permutation, non-orthogonal affine) so the outlier mass sits more favorably against the group scales, and only then round. Yet we are aware of no survey dedicated to this transform stage, and its literature is quietly re-deriving an older theory. We identify and formalize the principle that organizes it, the Great Inversion: allocation-flexible coding rewards energy concentration, whereas the grouped shared-scale quantization a deployed matrix instruction performs rewards within-group flattening. Classical transform coding (1963: decorrelate, allocate bits, quantize) spends different bits per coordinate at a fixed total rate; for a Gaussian source at high rate the Karhunen-Loeve transform's concentration minimizes distortion. A deployed operand tile instead carries one absolute-maximum scale per group and equal bits everywhere, with no allocation; on a uniform grid that objective rewards flattening, approached by Hadamard incoherence. We prove that opposition under within-group majorization: the prescriptions point in opposite directions, each backed by a proof against its own objective, and for a generic spectrum no optimality guarantee transfers. A second axis is the number format: the non-uniform FP4 grid makes flattening buy less, MXFP4's power-of-two block scale still rewards a rotation confined to that block, and NVFP4's mantissa-carrying scale largely removes that pull, so the target pole depends jointly on allocation regime and format. We survey 200 works to a June 2026 cutoff; classify 43 transform methods by structure, data-awareness, searched-versus-constructed, and runtime cost; record, where reported, how they compose with GPTQ rounding; distill a first-choice guide by deployment regime; and close with the open problems it exposes.

</details>


### [255] [GRAPE: Gradient Refinement and Progress-Aware Exploitation for Query-Efficient High-Dimensional Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25116)
*Richard Cornelius Suwandi,Feng Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GRAPE框架，通过梯度细化和进展感知优化，高效优化高维黑盒函数，在对抗攻击和LLM提示优化任务中显著提升查询效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有局部贝叶斯优化方法过于关注下降概率而忽视下降幅度，导致步长保守、改进微小，浪费查询资源。

Method: 两阶段框架：1）使用闭式采集函数锐化局部梯度后验；2）在条件下降前提下最大化期望下降量来选择更新方向。

Result: 理论证明梯度细化阶段单调降低局部不确定性，进展感知方向收敛至真实最速下降；实验显示在对抗攻击中加速5.4倍，LLM提示优化中最终平均 regret 降低3.8 log单位。

Conclusion: GRAPE通过平衡下降概率与下降幅度，显著提升高维黑盒函数优化的查询效率与性能。

Abstract: Optimizing expensive, high-dimensional black-box functions remains a central challenge in modern machine learning and scientific discovery. While local Bayesian optimization mitigates the curse of dimensionality, existing techniques often prioritize the probability of descent over the magnitude of progress. This leads to overly conservative steps that yield negligible improvement, wasting queries on directions that are nearly certain to descend but offer little decrease. We introduce Gradient Refinement and Progress-Aware Exploitation (GRAPE), a two-stage framework that first sharpens the local gradient posterior via a closed-form acquisition function, then selects update directions by maximizing the expected decrease conditional on descent. Theoretical analysis proves that this gradient refinement stage monotonically minimizes local uncertainty and that the progress-aware direction converges to true steepest descent as the posterior sharpens. Empirically, GRAPE demonstrates superior query efficiency across high-dimensional tasks: in black-box adversarial attacks, it achieves an average 5.4$\times$ speedup over baselines, and on large language model prompt optimization tasks, it outperforms the second best method by a reduction of 3.8 log-units in the final average regret.

</details>


### [256] [Demystifying Reinforcement Learning Post-Training of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24949)
*Donovan Clay,Saket Gollapudi,Sankar Harilal,Min Jang,Jacob Morrison,Sewoong Oh,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解构了大语言模型（LLM）强化学习（RL）后训练的黑盒机制，通过在可控环境中隔离变量（如基础模型先验、奖励粒度、提示分布及模型规模），利用策略输出熵分析预训练、SFT 及 RL 各阶段对模型确定性的影响，揭示了虚假奖励的效应依赖于提示分布，且 RL 成功与否取决于基础模型是否已在目标行为上分配了足够的概率质量（即探索问题）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 RL 后训练显著提升了 LLM 的推理、数学及编码能力，但其背后的经典 RL 原理对许多研究者和从业者而言仍是一个“黑盒”，缺乏对底层机制的清晰理解。

Method: 在受控且简化的环境中，利用可验证奖励（Verifiable Rewards）解构 RL 后训练算法的各个步骤；隔离并考察基础模型先验分布、奖励信号粒度、提示分布多样性及模型规模对结果的影响；以策略输出分布的熵为视角，对比分析预训练、SFT 和 RL 后训练三个阶段如何塑造模型的确定性。

Result: 研究发现：1. 所谓的“虚假奖励”（spurious rewards）的负面影响取决于后训练所使用的提示分布；2. RL 后训练的成功关键取决于基础模型是否已在期望行为上放置了足够的概率质量，这与经典 RL 中的“探索”概念直接相关；3. 阐明了各阶段选择如何相互作用从而影响后训练的最终成效。

Conclusion: 本文通过深入剖析 RL 后训练的内在机制，为 NLP 社区提供了一份实用的入门指南（primer），帮助研究者理解并将 RL 有效整合为提升模型能力的工具，明确了基础模型先验与数据分布选择在 RL 应用中的决定性作用。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has emerged as a powerful framework for enhancing the capabilities of large language models (LLMs), enabling impressive reasoning, math, and coding capabilities. Yet for many researchers and practitioners, the principles behind classical RL remain a "black box". In this work, we deconstruct the RL post-training algorithm, investigating each step to clarify what is actually happening beneath the surface. By isolating the mechanics of RL with Verifiable Rewards in a controlled and simplified environment, we examine how RL outcomes are shaped by the base model's prior distribution, the granularity of the reward signal, the diversity of the prompt distribution, and model scale. We use the entropy of the policy's output distribution as a lens to compare the distributions learned through pretraining, SFT, and RL post-training, revealing how each stage shapes model certainty. Our investigation sheds light on how these choices interact to affect post-training success. For example, we show that the effect of so-called 'spurious rewards' depends on the prompt distribution used for post-training. We also provide insight into why the success of RL post-training depends on whether the base model already places sufficient probability mass on the desired behavior, linking it to the classical concept of exploration in RL. Ultimately, we provide this primer as a resource to those in the NLP community wishing to incorporate RL as a tool in their toolbox.

</details>


### [257] [Agentic Autoresearch for Cell-Edge Power Control: Radically Redefining the Researcher's Role](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26093)
*Ahmad Khan,Akram Bin Sediq,Sara Azadegi Naeini,Raviraj S. Adve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用自主AI代理（autoresearch protocol）完全接管无线资源管理算法的设计过程，在无需人工干预的情况下，自动优化架构、损失函数及训练策略。实验表明，该代理在26小时内通过81次实验，以低600倍的推理成本达到了传统优化方法99.5%的性能，并自动发现了具有理论保证的max-min最优分配结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前为无线资源管理设计机器学习算法时高度依赖人工劳动的问题，包括架构选择、损失函数定义及训练配方均需手工指定，旨在将这一设计层完全交给自主智能体以实现自动化。

Method: 采用“自主研究协议”（autoresearch protocol）：由AI编码代理编辑训练脚本，执行固定预算的实验，并依据单一不可变指标决定保留或丢弃更改。代理被赋予对架构族、输入表示、输出参数化、损失函数及任务采样律的完全控制权，针对非凸、非平滑且NP-hard的多小区网络总和最低百分位率功率控制问题进行优化，并通过哈希固定评估器、强制推理契约和预注册证伪器等机制确保结果可信。

Result: 在26小时的81次无人值守实验中，代理在单次固定成本推理中达到了收敛的小化 - 最大化（minorization-maximization）参考基准99.5%的性能，推理成本降低约600倍，弥补了从首个可行架构起94%的性能差距；且单一参数集适用于所有网络规模和百分位目标。

Conclusion: 自主代理不仅实现了高性能，还恢复了可证明的数学结构而非仅仅调整常数：其发现的输出参数化在所有训练权重值下，均能精确复现最小百分位处的max-min最优分配，证明了AI自主设计在复杂无线资源管理任务中的有效性与理论深度。

Abstract: Designing machine learning algorithms for wireless resource management is labour-intensive: the architecture, the loss function and the training recipe are all specified by hand. We demonstrate that this design layer can be surrendered to an autonomous agent in its entirety. We adopt the autoresearch protocol, in which an AI coding agent edits a training script, runs a fixed-budget experiment, and retains or discards the change according to a single immutable metric. We grant the agent authority over the architecture family, the input representation, the output parameterization, the loss function and the task-sampling law, and set it a target chosen for its difficulty: sum-least-percentile-rate power control across a multicell network. The formulation targets cell-edge throughput and is non-convex, non-smooth and strongly NP-hard away from its max-min vertex. Safeguards render the results trustworthy: a hash-pinned evaluator, an enforced inference contract and a pre-registered falsifier per experiment. In eighty-one unattended experiments over twenty-six hours, the agent reached $99.5\%$ of a converged minorization-maximization reference in one fixed-cost inference pass, at roughly $600\times$ lower inference cost, closing $94\%$ of the gap from its first working architecture, with one parameter set serving every network size and percentile target. It recovered provable structure rather than tuned constants: the output parameterization it discovered reproduces the exact max-min-optimal allocation at the minimum percentile, for every value of the trained weights.

</details>


### [258] [Toward Machine Learning with the Unit as a Primitive: Learning from Unit-Linked Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25118)
*Heyang Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将“单元”（unit）作为机器学习任务语义中的显式原语，通过定义持久化指称对象及其同一性标准，将监督学习形式化为单元条件响应律的家族，并引入标记化器与共享响应律形式的学习对 $(T_φ,R_θ)$，以区分同质性与异质性世界，并解决单位未解析时的目标混合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习通常以样本形式化，但多个观测或可能事件所指向的持久化个体往往隐含未明，导致无法区分同质世界与异质世界的边际响应律，影响模型对真实世界结构的理解与泛化能力。

Method: 提出“单元”作为任务语义层面的显式原语，定义学习任务需声明持久化指称群体及同一性标准；监督学习被形式化为单元条件响应律家族；学习过程输出标记化器 $T_φ$ 与共享响应律形式 $R_θ$；引入“单元 abduction”从事实证据构建单元标记，或使用可信解析器提供查找标记。

Result: 形式化结果表明，在监督学习特化下，单元标记作为学习者侧表示，使任务侧单元影响预测；未链接的单行观测无法区分异质单元世界与同质池化世界，而可信的同单元对可提供受限见证以分离二者。

Conclusion: 显式建模“单元”可提升机器学习对世界结构的语义理解，区分同质性与异质性，解决单位未解析时的目标混合问题，为监督学习提供更坚实的语义基础与泛化保障。

Abstract: Machine learning is usually formalized through samples, while the persistent individual to which multiple observed or possible events refer often remains implicit. We propose the \emph{unit} as an explicit primitive at the level of task semantics. A learning task first declares a population of persistent referents and a sameness criterion; the realized value $u$ denotes the selected referent. Supervised learning is the main formal specialization. Its semantic object is a family of unit-conditioned response laws. Homogeneity is the special case in which those laws coincide; a sample-only conditional is silent as to whether the world is homogeneous or the observed law is only the marginal of a heterogeneous family. What is learned from data is a pair $(T_φ,R_θ)$: a tokenizer that produces a contextual unit token and one shared response-law form that reads it. The structured class takes that form to be a simple relation in the token; a linear predictor is the running instance. The token is the learner-side representation through which the task-side unit affects prediction, while a learner specification that omits unit information is unit-insensitive; homogeneity remains a property of the world-side response family. When identity is unresolved, the world-side law mixes unit-conditioned targets, while the learner composes its shared form with a token. A trusted resolver may fix the unit and supply a lookup token; otherwise \emph{unit abduction} forms a token of the same type from factual evidence. Unlinked single-row observations can fail to distinguish a heterogeneous unit world from a homogeneous pooled world; trusted same-unit pairs separate a restricted witness. The formal results concern this supervised specialization.

</details>


### [259] [AFDBench: A Reasoning-First AI Scientist for NationalWeather Service Forecast Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24954)
*Manmeet Singh,Somnath Luitel,Prabhjot Singh,Manraaj Banga,Naveen Sudharsan,Josh Durkee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AFDBench基准和AI气象学家模型，通过强化学习解决大语言模型在生成高利害气象文本时的数值幻觉问题，显著提升专业性和数据忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在生成高利害气象文本时会产生数值幻觉，对天气传播构成风险，需要评估和提升生成式气象推理能力。

Method: 构建AFDBench基准（7,732篇专家撰写的讨论），引入三个评估指标（Met-Align、Style-Align、Input-Grounding），并应用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)结合领域特定奖励进行强化学习训练。

Result: 零样本评估显示开源LLMs的Style-Align较低(~0.33)，Input-Grounding中等(~0.88)；经GRPO优化后，Style-Align从0.318提升至0.619，Input-Grounding从0.881提升至0.940。

Conclusion: 强化学习能有效教导7B参数模型像专业气象学家一样写作并忠实解释AI天气数据，验证了该方法在气象文本生成中的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) hallucinate numerical values when generating high-stakes meteorological text, posing risks for weather communication. We present AFDBench, an AI meteorologist that generates professional Area Forecast Discussions (AFDs) by reasoning through structured AI weather forecast data from Google's WeatherNext 2. We introduce AFDBench, the first benchmark for evaluating generative meteorological reasoning, comprising 7,732 expert written discussions from 13 National Weather Service (NWS) offices paired with real AI weather forecast inputs, and three complementary metrics: Met-Align (numerical accuracy), Style-Align (professional dialect adherence), and Input-Grounding (fidelity to source weather data). Zero-shot evaluations reveal that open-source LLMs achieve low Style-Align (~0.33) and moderate Input-Grounding (~0.88), failing to write in the professional NWS register or faithfully use their input data. We apply Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with domain-specific rewards targeting temperature accuracy, synoptic correctness, and format compliance. On 1,033 held-out samples from two unseen NWS offices, GRPO nearly doubles Style-Align from 0.318 to 0.619 and improves Input-Grounding from 0.881 to 0.940, demonstrating that reinforcement learning teaches a 7B-parameter model to write like a professional meteorologist and faithfully interpret AI weather data.

</details>


### [260] [Representing MAX functions using two-hidden-layer ReLU networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25221)
*Zhimao Wang,Amitabh Basu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过计算机辅助搜索，构建了N=5至8的MAX函数的两层ReLU神经网络精确表示，填补了相关理论空白，并独立于近期其他研究得出了类似结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在表征表示连续分段线性函数所需的精确隐藏层数量。目前该问题的下界为2，上界为N的对数，而是否仅需常数层（甚至2层）仍完全未决。此前研究仅解决了MAX_5，N≥6的情况尚属开放问题。

Method: 采用仔细的计算机辅助搜索，寻找形如$\max\{\sum\max(x_{a_r},x_{b_r}),\sum\max(x_{c_r},x_{d_r})\}$的有理线性组合恒等式。利用第一隐藏层计算坐标对的内层最大值，第二隐藏层计算两侧和的外层最大值，从而构造出两层ReLU实现。

Result: 成功获得了$\mathrm{MAX}_5, \mathrm{MAX}_6, \mathrm{MAX}_7$和$\mathrm{MAX}_8$的两层ReLU精确表示。这些表示形式与Ruess等人（2026）近期独立得出的结果（覆盖N≤10）不同，但在高层思想上有共通之处。

Conclusion: 证实了对于N≤8的情况，MAX函数确实可以通过两层ReLU网络精确表示，为“常数层数”假说提供了新的实证支持，并提供了不同于现有文献的构造方法，对后续研究具有参考价值。

Abstract: We study exact representations of $\mathrm{MAX}_N(x)=\max{x_1,\ldots,x_N}$ using two-hidden-layer ReLU neural networks. This problem has been studied in recent years in an attempt to characterize the exact number of hidden layers required to represent continuous piecewise linear functions. The best lower bound is 2, while the current upper bound is logarithmic in $N$. It remains completely open if the right answer is a constant number of hidden layers (possibly even 2!) or not. In fact, a recent breakthrough was the representation of $\mathrm{MAX}_5$ as a two-hidden-layer ReLU function obtained in [Bakaev et al., 2026], and the case of $\mathrm{MAX}_N$ was stated as open for $N\geq 6$ in that paper.
  Using a careful computer assisted search, we obtain two-hidden-layer ReLU representations of $\mathrm{MAX}_5, \mathrm{MAX}_6, \mathrm{MAX}_7$, and $\mathrm{MAX}_8$. We obtain these by considering rational linear combinations of terms of the form $\max\{\sum_{r=1}^{s}\max(x_{a_r},x_{b_r}),\sum_{r=1}^{s}\max(x_{c_r},x_{d_r})\}$, where $a_r,b_r,c_r,d_r\in\{1,\ldots,N\}$. Each inner maximum of two coordinates can be computed in a first hidden layer, and the outer maximum of the two side-sums can be computed in a second hidden layer. Consequently, every finite linear combination of these terms has a two-hidden-layer ReLU realization. An identity for $\mathrm{MAX}_N$ in this form therefore gives an exact two-hidden-layer ReLU representation of $\mathrm{MAX}_N$.
  Very recently, two-hidden-layer representations of $\mathrm{MAX}_N$ of the above form were obtained for all $N\leq 10$ in [Ruess et al., 2026]. Our representations are different and were developed independently. While our techniques share most of the high-level ideas presented in [Ruess et al., 2026], there are also some minor differences which may be of interest for future research on this problem.

</details>


### [261] [Why and When Neural Networks Improve Local Approximation in Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24963)
*Chengkuo Bian,Pengcheng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了神经代理模型在无导数优化中效果矛盾的原因，指出关键在于代理的“角色”（辅助提议还是替代梯度）、“半径”（局部邻域可靠性）和“空间”（基础方法的剩余优化潜力），而非单纯的拟合精度。实验表明，正确配置这三要素可显著提升求解成功率，反之则导致性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有文献中关于神经代理模型在无导数优化（derivative-free optimisation）中表现不一致的矛盾现象：同一类模型在某些求解器中能减少评估次数，而在其他求解器中却无效甚至产生负面影响。

Method: 提出并形式化了三个决定性因素：角色（Role，区分辅助提议与梯度替代）、半径（Radius，基于半径感知的局部泛化及完全线性条件）、空间（Room，基础方法的剩余优化能力）。在固定代理类别、训练流程和基础方法的前提下，通过117个基准实例、不同噪声水平的配对比较以及蒙特卡洛库存模型进行实证测试。

Result: 1. 辅助提议模式将高精度求解实例从67提升至84，而梯度替代模式降至65；2. 移除训练损失中的梯度项使代理接受率从0.703骤降至0.148；3. 噪声水平无特定阈值，性能瓶颈在于基础方法过早停止；4. 在基于模型的信任域求解器中，因缺乏优化空间，附加代理导致性能从88降至86；5. 在库存模型中，修复接受接口的价值（10.40成本单位）远高于直接引入代理（0.00）。

Conclusion: 神经代理模型的有效性取决于其角色定位、局部泛化半径及基础方法的优化空间，而非训练曲线的拟合精度。只有当代理作为辅助提议者、在可靠邻域内运行且基础方法仍有优化潜力时，才能带来实质性增益；否则可能适得其反。

Abstract: Published experience with neural surrogates in derivative-free optimisation is contradictory: the same family of models that cuts the evaluation count of one solver leaves another unchanged, or makes it worse. We show that the contradiction dissolves once three factors are stated, and that these, rather than the fit accuracy a training curve reports, are what delimit when a learned local model pays. Role: a surrogate that proposes candidates the true objective must still approve helps, while one that replaces a gradient the solver depends on hurts. Radius: a model fitted to an optimisation path is reliable only inside a bounded neighbourhood, and its error neither vanishes as that neighbourhood shrinks nor survives its growth. Room: a surrogate can only accelerate progress the base method is still able to make. We formalise radius-aware local generalisation, relate it to the classical fully linear condition, and test each factor with the surrogate class, training pipeline and base method held fixed. Over 117 benchmark instances safeguarded assistance raises the instances solved to high accuracy from 67 to 84 while gradient replacement lowers them to 65; removing the gradient term from the training loss cuts surrogate acceptance from 0.703 to 0.148; and 1000 paired comparisons over ten noise levels show no noise threshold, only a base method that stops early. The same factors bound the gain: a model-based trust-region solver, which leaves little room, drops from 88 to 86 when the identical surrogate is attached, and released interpolation software stays ahead at 103, and on a Monte-Carlo inventory model repairing the acceptance interface is worth 10.40 cost units against 0.00 for the surrogate.

</details>


### [262] [Physics-Informed Error Field Learning: A Post-Training Optimization Framework for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24970)
*Jiuyun Sun,Yong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出物理信息误差场学习（PIEFL）框架，通过引入辅助误差网络修正PINNs的预测残差，在不改变主网络架构的前提下，以更低计算成本显著提升求解偏微分方程的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决PINNs在优化后期参数更新带来的精度提升边际效应递减及计算成本增加的问题，避免对整个解空间进行持续低效的优化。

Method: 提出PIEFL框架：在主网络达到满意精度后，引入辅助误差网络；推导物理约束下的误差控制方程，使误差网络学习当前近似解与精确解之间的残差；将学习到的误差修正项与主网络预测值叠加，实现精度提升。

Result: 在代表性偏微分方程的数值实验中，PIEFL在同等计算预算下实现了比传统方法更高的解精度，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: PIEFL通过将学习目标从解场转移至误差场，有效集中计算资源修正预测误差，作为一种通用的后训练优化策略，兼容现有PINN模型并显著提升了其性能。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) have emerged as an important class of numerical methods for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). However, during the late-stage optimization process, further parameter updates often yield diminishing accuracy improvements while increasing computational costs. To address this issue, this paper proposes a Physics-Informed Error Field Learning (PIEFL) framework for PINNs. Unlike conventional approaches that continuously approximate the solution field using a single network, PIEFL introduces an auxiliary error network after the primary network achieves satisfactory accuracy and shifts the learning objective from the solution field to the error field. By deriving error control equations under physical constraints, the error network learns the discrepancy between the current approximation and the exact solution, and the learned error correction is combined with the primary prediction to improve solution accuracy. The proposed framework avoids continuous optimization of the entire solution space and focuses computational resources on correcting existing prediction errors. Moreover, PIEFL requires no modification to the primary network architecture, making it compatible with existing PINN models and applicable as a general post-training optimization strategy. Numerical experiments on representative PDEs demonstrate that PIEFL achieves higher solution accuracy under the same computational budget, validating its effectiveness in improving the performance of PINNs.

</details>


### [263] [Beyond Optimal Rates in Stochastic Optimization: Trajectory-Adaptive Stopping Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25551)
*Liviu Aolaritei,Lucas Lévy,Francis Bach,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对强凸随机优化问题，提出了一种基于轨迹自适应的上置信序列方法，解决了随机梯度下降（SGD）在数据依赖停止时间下的统计有效性认证难题，实现了无需牺牲统计有效性的自适应早停，且迭代次数显著少于传统确定性视界方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SGD分析通常基于预先设定的确定性视界，而实际应用中停止决策是自适应的（基于演化的轨迹）。这种不匹配导致固定时间的理论保证在数据依赖的停止时间下不再有效，而基于最坏情况边界推导的确定性视界又过于保守。

Method: 构建完全可观测的、轨迹自适应的上置信序列，用于衡量最后迭代点与优化器的平方距离及加权平均的次优性；开发新的递归置信序列技术及适用于具有时变条件均值和无界可预测范围的自适应过程的一般化时间均匀经验伯恩斯坦不等式；并将该方法扩展至小批量SGD，利用小批量内的 realized 二阶矩结构。

Result: 所提出的界限在所有时间点上同时成立，在最坏情况下达到最优的 $1/t$ 衰减率（至多相差迭代对数因子），并能适应实际实现的随机梯度；数值实验表明，由此产生的停止规则所需的迭代次数比自然确定性视界少几个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过将演化的SGD轨迹视为提供关于未知优化误差证据的序贯实验，本文成功解决了SGD的自适应停止认证问题，使得算法能在达到预设精度时立即停止，同时保持统计有效性并大幅提升计算效率。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is typically analyzed at a deterministic horizon chosen before the algorithm is run, even though practical stopping decisions are made adaptively by inspecting the evolving trajectory. This mismatch creates a fundamental certification problem: fixed-time guarantees do not generally remain valid at data-dependent stopping times, while deterministic horizons derived from worst-case bounds can be highly conservative. We address this problem for strongly convex stochastic optimization by constructing fully observable, trajectory-adaptive upper confidence sequences for the squared distance of the last iterate to the optimizer and the suboptimality of a weighted average. These bounds hold simultaneously over time, attain the optimal $1/t$ decay rate up to iterated-logarithmic factors in the worst case, and adapt to the realized stochastic gradients, allowing SGD to stop as soon as a prescribed accuracy is certified without sacrificing statistical validity. Our approach treats the evolving SGD trajectory as a sequential experiment whose observations provide evidence about the unknown optimization error. To formalize this perspective, we develop new recursive confidence-sequence techniques and a general time-uniform empirical Bernstein inequality for adapted processes with time-varying conditional means and predictable ranges that may grow without bound. We further extend these confidence-sequence constructions to minibatch SGD, with the empirical Bernstein bounds exploiting the realized second-moment structure within each minibatch. Numerical experiments show that the resulting stopping rules can require several orders of magnitude fewer iterations than natural deterministic horizons.

</details>


### [264] [Resource-Efficient Pruning for Transformer via Low-Rank Importance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24973)
*Peng Liu,Huibing Zeng,Yiqun Zhang,Yang Yi,Jigang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出REP-LIE方法，利用LoRA低秩矩阵梯度估算权重重要性，在微调过程中实现资源高效的模型剪枝，无需全梯度计算和全参数优化，在多个模型上取得竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的大规模预训练语言模型计算和内存成本高，阻碍了在资源受限环境中的部署。传统剪枝方法依赖全梯度重要性估计，需要预先微调模型，导致不可接受的资源消耗。

Method: 提出REP-LIE方法，利用LoRA低秩矩阵的梯度来估算权重重要性，避免全梯度计算；引入稳定性分数解决重要性估计的随机性问题，作为迭代剪枝的基础；剪枝后通过轻量级更新进行微调，无需全参数优化。

Result: 在中等规模编码器模型和大规模生成模型（LLaMA-7B和Mistral-7B）上的实验表明，REP-LIE与现有方法相比仍能取得竞争性性能。

Conclusion: REP-LIE能够在微调过程中实现资源高效的模型剪枝，显著降低计算和内存成本，同时保持模型性能，适用于资源受限环境的模型部署。

Abstract: With the rapid development of large-scale pre-trained language models based on Transformer architectures, their high computational and memory costs have become a major obstacle to deployment, especially in resource-constrained environments. Traditional pruning methods typically depend on full gradient-based importance estimation, and they necessitate prior finetuning of the model to achieve satisfactory performance. This process often results in intolerable resource consumption. This paper proposes REP-LIE, a new approach to enable resource-efficient pruning during the process of finetuning. REP-LIE leverages the gradients of LoRA low-rank matrices to estimate the importance of weights without requiring full gradient computation. To address the inherent randomness in importance estimation, a stability score is introduced, serving as the basis for iterative pruning of unimportant model parameters. The pruned model is further finetuned through lightweight updates, eliminating the need for full-parameter optimization in the process of finetuning. Extensive experiments on both medium-scale encoder models and large-scale generative models (LLaMA-7B and Mistral-7B) demonstrate that REP-LIE still achieves competitive performance compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [265] [Comparing Corrupted Constrained Learning Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25745)
*Laura Iacovissi,Rabanus Derr,Robert C. Williamson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出经典数据处理不等式在机器学习受限假设类场景下失效，提出了广义数据处理不等式，并将其等价转化为超预测集（superprediction set）的包含条件，最后推导了该包含关系的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典数据处理不等式（Data Processing Inequality）表明随机修改实验不会降低贝叶斯风险，但该结论假设模型类包含所有可测函数。然而，机器学习本质上是受限学习问题（constrained learning problems），使用的模型类并不包含所有函数，导致经典不等式在此场景下不再成立，亟需修正理论以适配机器学习实际。

Method: 1. 构造反例证明经典数据处理不等式在受限假设类设置下失效；2. 提出广义数据处理不等式，要求联合分布的受限贝叶斯风险下界于随机修改分布的受限贝叶斯风险；3. 将该不等式等价转化为由损失函数和模型类诱导的特定函数集（即超预测集）的集合包含条件；4. 推导该集合包含关系成立的充分条件。

Result: 成功证明了经典数据处理不等式在受限学习场景中的局限性，建立了广义数据处理不等式框架，并揭示了该不等式与超预测集包含条件之间的等价性，同时给出了保证该包含关系成立的充分条件。

Conclusion: 在机器学习受限模型类的背景下，必须采用广义数据处理不等式替代经典形式；该广义不等式的成立取决于超预测集的几何包含性质，这为理解信息瓶颈原理及特征学习技术在受限设置下的理论基础提供了新的视角和判据。

Abstract: A key result in statistics is the data processing inequality, originally proved by Blackwell (1951) and later refined by DeGroot (1962) in terms of statistical uncertainty. It states that the Bayes risk of a statistical experiment obtained by stochastically modifying another experiment cannot be lower than the Bayes risk of the original experiment, regardless of the loss function or prior chosen. In machine learning, this result underlies applications such as the information bottleneck principle and some feature learning techniques. However, machine learning problems are constrained learning problems: the model class used does not include all measurable functions. We present a simple counterexample showing that the classical data processing inequality fails to hold in such a setting. Hence, we formulate a generalized data processing inequality, requiring the constrained Bayes risk of a joint distribution (with respect to a loss function and a constrained hypothesis class) to lower bound the constrained Bayes risk on the stochastically modified distribution, regardless of the choice of distribution. We show this inequality to be equivalent to a set containment condition on a specific function set induced by the loss and model class, called the superprediction set. Finally, we derive sufficient conditions for this containment.

</details>


### [266] [Clearing the Underbrush: AI-Enhanced RF Interference Suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24974)
*Rahul Jain,Pierre Trepagnier,Rick Gentile,Joey Botero,Alexia Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合有限标量量化（FSQ）标记器层的自回归Transformer模型，旨在以最低延迟显著提升射频信号中的结构化干扰抑制性能，并通过音频指标验证了其在实际场景中的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的干扰抑制方法在性能上往往不及深度学习方法，后者能联合考虑感兴趣信号（SOI）与混合信号。然而，现有的AI方法仍需在推理速度（延迟）与抑制精度之间寻求更好的平衡，以满足实际操作需求。

Method: 在先前基于自回归Transformer的AI框架基础上，引入有限标量量化（FSQ）标记器层以优化模型架构；同时实验了多种推理优化技术。实验设置中，SOI为数字调制射频信号，结构化干扰为常见的数字电视信号（OFDM传输）。

Result: 该方法实现了低延迟，并在干扰抑制性能上超越了传统技术及先前的AI方法。通过感知语音质量评估（PESQ）等音频指标，证实了AI赋能方法的有效性及音质提升。

Conclusion: 引入FSQ标记器及推理优化技术的AI干扰抑制算法，能够在保持极低延迟的同时显著提高性能，适用于多种操作相关的实际应用场景，展现了其在射频信号处理领域的巨大潜力。

Abstract: AI-based structured interference rejection has grown more popular because deep learning approaches can outperform traditional methods by jointly considering the signal of interest (SOI) and the signal mixture (SOI plus interference). This work builds on a previous AI-enabled approach utilizing autoregressive transformer-based models by adding a Finite Scalar Quantization (FSQ) tokenizer layer which aims to improve the interference rejection performance while keeping overall latency to a minimum. Additionally, we experiment with other inference optimization techniques with the goal of speeding up inference without much accuracy loss. We explore this space with an experiment where the SOI is a digitally modulated radio frequency (RF) signal and the structured interference is a digital television signal, an extremely common type of Orthogonal Frequency-Division Multiplexing (OFDM) transmission. Our results achieve low latency and increased interference rejection over traditional techniques and prior work with other AI-enabled methods. We demonstrate the benefits of the AI-enabled approaches via audio metrics such as Perceptual Evaluation of Speech Quality (PESQ). Additionally, we explore a variety of applications and detail how our interference rejection algorithm may be used in operationally-relevant scenarios.

</details>


### [267] [Geometry-Constrained Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks: Learning Edge Geometry via Banach Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25807)
*K S Sesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何约束 KAN（Banach-KAN），通过引入可学习的标量指数 $p$ 动态调整边缘激活函数的几何形态，在符号回归任务中（特别是含噪声和小样本场景下）性能优于或持平于基于固定基（如样条、多项式）的现有 KAN 变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 KAN 变体依赖固定的基函数（如样条、多项式或傅里叶特征），这在数据观测前就强加了固定的函数空间几何结构，限制了模型的适应性。

Method: 引入基于 Banach 对偶映射的几何约束 KAN，为每条边学习一个标量指数 $p > 1$ 来控制几何形态：$p < 2$ 产生类似 $\ell_1$ 的锐利阈值行为，$p = 2$ 恢复线性，$p > 2$ 则在原点附近产生更平坦的响应。

Result: 在 50 个符号回归目标（含 AI Feynman 基准及合成压力测试）上，Banach-KAN 的中位 NRMSE 为 0.030，优于样条并持平切比雪夫基；在噪声鲁棒性上显著优于未正则化样条（退化倍数 8.8x vs 21.6x）；在小样本 regime 下每方程胜率最高。

Conclusion: 学习到的指数 $p$ 提供了可解释的几何信号，揭示了不同方程族和输入维度间一致的几何排序；该方法在噪声环境和数据稀缺时表现出更强的稳定性和适应性，无需预先固定函数空间几何。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) replace fixed activations in deep architectures with learnable univariate edge functions, making the choice of edge parametrisation central. Existing variants rely on fixed bases such as splines, polynomials, or Fourier features, which impose a function-space geometry before data are observed. We introduce geometry-constrained KANs, a family of edge activations derived from Banach duality maps in which the geometry itself is learned through a scalar exponent $p > 1$ per edge. This exponent controls the qualitative response: sub-Euclidean values produce sharp, threshold-like behaviour reminiscent of the $\ell_1$ (LASSO) geometry, $p = 2$ recovers the linear regime, and larger values produce flatter responses near the origin. Across 50 symbolic-regression targets ($40$ from the AI Feynman benchmark plus $10$ synthetic stress tests), geometry-constrained KANs match or beat every fixed-basis baseline on median NRMSE (Banach-KAN $0.030$, tying Chebyshev and improving on splines); on average rank Banach-KAN is best on the $18$-equation core ($2.00$) and statistically tied with the strongest spline on the full benchmark ($2.32$ vs. $2.34$). The clearest gains appear under measurement noise: as $σ$ grows from $0$ to $1$, $\ell^p$-KAN degrades only $3.7\times$ -- below even a cross-validated spline ($\approx 11\times$) -- while an unregularised spline degrades $21.6\times$; Banach-KAN degrades $8.8\times$, comparable to a tuned spline but far more stable than the unregularised one. Banach-KAN also takes the most per-equation wins in the small-sample regime, with fixed-basis models catching up only as the training set grows. Learned exponents provide an interpretable, relative signal: at a fixed initialisation they reveal a consistent, target-dependent geometric ordering across equation families and input dimensions.

</details>


### [268] [MSR-IVA: Masked Structural Residual Independent Vector Analysis for State-Aware Fusion of Structural MRI and Dynamic Functional Network Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24978)
*Victor Solomon,Zening Fu,Rafal Angryk,Vince D. Calhoun,Jingyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MSR-IVA的新框架，用于多模态融合结构MRI和动态功能网络连通性，通过结合共享结构表示与状态特定残差适应，解决了现有方法在状态间结构分解不一致或过度约束的问题，并在阿尔茨海默病数据集上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的独立向量分析（IVA）方法在处理多模态脑影像数据时存在局限：对每个动态状态单独应用IVA会导致结构分解不相关，而强制相同分解则会抑制状态特异性关系；此外，并非所有受试者都表达每个动态状态，现有方法难以处理这种不完整状态表达。

Method: 提出掩蔽结构残差IVA（MSR-IVA）框架，该框架结合共享结构表示与状态特定残差适应，并引入掩蔽机制处理不完整状态表达，实现状态感知的多模态融合分析。

Result: 在阿尔茨海默病神经影像倡议（ADNI）队列上，MSR-IVA相比独立成对IVA基线，匹配源耦合提高了6.5%，不匹配依赖性降低了15.7%；在表达两种状态的受试者中，MSR-IVA的跨状态结构源平均绝对相关性为0.9177，而无共享方法仅为0.2978。

Conclusion: MSR-IVA框架能够有效控制结构共享，在保持源对应关系的同时允许状态特定适应，为多模态脑影像数据的动态状态分析提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal fusion of structural MRI (sMRI) and dynamic functional network connectivity (dFNC) can reveal how brain structure relates to changing functional states. When the same structural latent representation is coupled with multiple states, applying independent vector analysis (IVA) separately to each state can produce unrelated structural decompositions, while forcing identical decompositions may suppress state-specific relationships. In addition, not every subject expresses every dynamic state. We propose masked structural residual IVA (MSR-IVA), a state-aware framework that combines a shared structural representation with state-specific residual adaptations and masks for incomplete state expression. On an Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative cohort, MSR-IVA improved matched source coupling by 6.5% and reduced unmatched dependence by 15.7% relative to the independent pairwise IVA baseline. Among subjects expressing both states, mean absolute cross-state structural source correlation was 0.9177 for MSR-IVA versus 0.2978 for no sharing, demonstrating controlled structural sharing that preserves source correspondence while allowing state-specific adaptation.

</details>


### [269] [Quantum-Inspired Modeling of Driving Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25907)
*Mohammad Elayan,Omid Armantalab,Wissam Kontar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受量子力学启发的驾驶员行为表示方法，通过演化密度矩阵统一建模行为的不确定性、时间演变及上下文依赖性，在无监督训练下成功识别出三种可解释的驾驶状态（自由流、过渡、拥堵），并能复现宏观交通现象及支持自动驾驶预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶员行为模型通常预先固定变量交互方式，导致无法捕捉模型形式之外的行为（被视为噪声），而过于灵活的模型又往往缺乏可解释性。需要一种既能捕捉异构、随时间变化且依赖上下文的驾驶员行为，又能保持可解释性的统一框架。

Method: 引入受量子启发的表示法，将每位驾驶员编码为演化的密度矩阵（evolving density matrix）。该方法在 I-24 MOTION 数据集上进行无监督训练，能够连续、概率化地学习行为变量间的交互，并捕捉历史依赖和上下文依赖。

Result: 1. 恢复了三种可解释的驾驶剖面（自由流、过渡、拥堵），捕捉了数据的行为范围及状态间的平滑转换；2. 复现了已知的宏观现象，包括基本图（fundamental diagram）和滞后回环（hysteresis loops）；3. 可为经典跟驰模型提供上下文相关参数，并为自动驾驶车辆提供周围驾驶员的实时行为读取及短时运动预测。

Conclusion: 该框架通过构造实现了交通模型的可解释性与可信度，证明了量子灵感表示法在统一微观行为不确定性与宏观交通现象方面的有效性，并已开源相关工具包以支持数据处理、训练、推理及分析。

Abstract: Driver behavior is heterogeneous, context-dependent, and changes over time, and these properties shape the traffic phenomena we observe. Most models, however, fix in advance which behavioral variables interact and how. Behavior outside that form is absorbed as noise, while models flexible enough to capture it tend to lose interpretability. We introduce a quantum-inspired representation of driver behavior that combines properties usually treated separately or in part: it is continuous, probabilistic, context-dependent, history-dependent, and represents interactions among behavioral variables as learned from data. Each driver is encoded as an evolving density matrix, providing a unified representation of behavioral uncertainty, temporal evolution, and context-dependent behavioral variation. Trained without supervision on the I-24 MOTION dataset, the framework recovers three interpretable driving profiles representing three regimes: free flow, transition, and congestion. The profiles capture the behavioral range of the data and the smooth transitions drivers make between regimes as conditions change. The same representation also reproduces known macroscopic phenomena, aligning with the fundamental diagram and reproducing hysteresis loops. We also show how the representation supports practical use: it supplies context-dependent parameters to classical car-following models, and gives an autonomous vehicle a live behavioral read of the surrounding drivers with a short-horizon forecast of their motion. The framework points toward models of traffic that are interpretable and trustworthy by construction. We release an open-source toolkit on GitHub (https://github.com/mselayan/quantum-driver-representation) spanning data processing, training, inference, and analysis.

</details>


### [270] [D$^3$-MOPD: Adaptive Dynamic Domain ScheDuling for Efficient Multi-Teacher Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24987)
*Zechen Sun,Zhiwei Zhang,Fei Zhao,Juntao Li,Mu Chuan,Huayu Deng,Guojian Zhan,Wenliang Chen,Yao Hu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出D³-MOPD，一种用于多教师策略蒸馏的动态域调度方法，通过在线调整域数据混合比例，解决了固定混合导致计算资源浪费和慢收敛域训练不足的问题，显著提升了学生模型的性能和训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多教师策略蒸馏（MOPD）方法通常在训练前固定各域的数据混合比例，忽略了不同域收敛速度差异巨大的事实：部分域过早 plateau，而部分域在整个训练预算内持续改进。这种固定混合导致计算资源在快收敛域上被浪费，而在慢收敛域上训练不足。

Method: 提出D³-MOPD（Dynamic Domain ScheDuling for MOPD），这是一种零开销调度器。它复用训练过程中产生的每域反向KL散度信号，在训练过程之外异步运行一个“观察者”进程，定期追踪各域的KL轨迹，估算剩余提升空间和当前改进速率，并据此动态调整域采样比例，无需改变核心训练循环。

Result: 在从四个域专家教师蒸馏到Qwen3.6-35B-A3B学生模型的实验中，D³-MOPD缩小了97%的平均学生与教师性能差距（ vanilla MOPD仅为63%）；在减少约3倍rollout步数的情况下达到相同峰值性能；并在七个基准测试中的三个上超越了专家教师。

Conclusion: D³-MOPD通过动态调度有效利用了不同域的收敛模式差异，不仅大幅提升了蒸馏效率和最终性能，且其优势随域数量增加而增强，具有自然扩展性。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) distills several domain-expert teachers into a single student by minimizing per-domain reverse-KL divergence on the student's own rollouts.
